### [38] [A Semantic-Sampling Framework for Evaluating Calibration in Open-Ended Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08432)
*Zhanliang Wang,Jiancong Xiao,Ruochen Jin,Shu Yang,Bojian Hou,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Sem-ECE，一种用于开放性问答中大语言模型校准评估的新框架，通过语义采样和分组估计置信度，在多个基准和主流大模型上优于现有方法，并揭示了问题难度的诊断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的校准评估方法在开放性问答场景中存在局限，如依赖受限输出格式、自报告置信度或特定任务规则，缺乏适用于真实场景的可靠评估手段。

Method: 提出Sem-ECE框架，包含两种估计器Sem_1-ECE和Sem_2-ECE，通过对模型生成的答案进行语义聚类并利用频率作为置信度来评估校准误差，并证明其渐近无偏性。

Result: 实验表明Sem-ECE在三个开放性问答基准和五个主流商业大模型上优于口头化置信度和现有采样方法，且Sem_2-ECE在校准误差上更优，二者差异可作问题难度诊断。

Conclusion: Sem-ECE为开放性问答中的大语言模型校准提供了更可靠、实用的评估方案，尤其在无法获取内部概率时具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Calibration measures whether a model's predicted confidence aligns with its empirical accuracy, and is central to the reliable deployment of large language models (LLMs) in high-stakes domains such as medicine and law. While much recent work focuses on improving LLM calibration, the equally important question of how to evaluate it in realistic settings remains underdeveloped. Open-ended question answering (QA), the most common deployment setting for modern LLMs, is where existing evaluation methods fall short: logit-based metrics need restricted output formats and internal probabilities; verbalized confidence is self-reported and often overconfident; and sampling-based methods rely on task-specific extraction rules without a clear finite-sample target. We introduce Sem-ECE (Semantic-Sampling Expected Calibration Error), a calibration evaluation framework for open-ended QA that samples answers from the model, groups them into semantic classes, and uses the resulting frequencies as confidence. We study two estimators within this framework: Sem$_1$-ECE, the same-sample self-consistency score, and Sem$_2$-ECE, a held-out variant that separates answer selection from confidence evaluation. We prove both are asymptotically unbiased, and further show that they agree on easy questions but diverge on hard ones with Sem$_2$ achieving strictly smaller calibration error, so their gap also serves as a diagnostic for question difficulty. Experiments on three open-ended QA benchmarks across five leading commercial LLMs match our theoretical predictions and show that Sem-ECE outperforms verbalized confidence and existing sampling-based methods, while complementing logit-based evaluation when internal probabilities are unavailable.

</details>


### [39] [Magis-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Magistrate-Level Legal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08437)
*Ramon Pires,Thales Sales Almeida,Celio Larcher Junior,Giovana Bonás,Hugo Abonizio,Marcos Piau,Roseval Malaquias Junior,Thiago Laitz,Rodrigo Nogueira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Magis-Bench，一个基于巴西司法职位竞争性考试的裁判级法律写作任务基准，用于评估大语言模型在司法推理和文书撰写方面的能力。研究评估了23个最先进模型的表现，发现当前模型仍远未达到人类水平，表明司法级别的法律推理对现有LLM仍是挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的法律AI基准主要关注生成法律论据或文件的任务，而判断论据、权衡主张并作出合理裁决的能力同样关键。因此，需要一个专注于裁判型法律写作能力的基准来更全面地评估LLM在法律领域的实际表现。

Method: 构建了一个名为Magis-Bench的新基准，包含2023至2025年间八次巴西司法考试中的74道题目，涵盖多轮结构的论述题和完整的民事、刑事判决书撰写任务；采用LLM-as-a-judge方法，使用四个前沿模型作为独立评分者进行评估。

Result: 四个评分模型之间表现出高度一致（Kendall's W = 0.984；成对Kendall's τ ≥ 0.897）；Gemini-3-Pro-Preview得分最高（6.97/10），其次是Gemini-3-Flash-Preview（6.67）和Claude-4.5-Opus（6.46）；但最佳模型得分仍低于满分的70%。

Conclusion: 当前的大语言模型在 magistrate-level 的法律推理与写作任务上仍有显著不足，司法级别的法律判断能力仍是重大挑战，需进一步研究提升。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for legal AI focus primarily on tasks where LLMs must produce legal arguments or documents, yet the capacity to \emph{judge} such arguments -- weighing competing claims, applying doctrine to facts, and rendering reasoned decisions -- is arguably as fundamental to a well-functioning legal system as advocacy itself. We introduce Magis-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs on magistrate-level writing tasks derived from recent Brazilian competitive examinations for judicial positions. Magis-Bench comprises 74 questions from eight examinations conducted between 2023 and 2025, including discursive legal analysis questions with multi-turn structure and practical exercises requiring the composition of complete civil and criminal judicial sentences. We evaluate 23 state-of-the-art LLMs using an LLM-as-a-judge methodology with four independent frontier models as evaluators. Our results show strong inter-judge agreement (Kendall's $W = 0.984$; pairwise Kendall's $τ\ge 0.897$), with Google's Gemini-3-Pro-Preview achieving the highest average score (6.97/10), followed by Gemini-3-Flash-Preview (6.67) and Claude-4.5-Opus (6.46). Even the best-performing models score below 70\% of the maximum, indicating that judicial-level legal reasoning and writing remain challenging for current LLMs. We release the complete benchmark, model outputs, and evaluation code to support further research on legal AI capabilities.

</details>

*Natalie Seah,Danielle S. Bitterman,Daphna Spiegel,Thomas Hartvigsen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种面向部署的压力测试框架，用于评估大型语言模型（LLM）在乳腺癌放疗副作用生成中的可靠性。通过21个患者档案和临床场景对比，发现LLM对文档微小变化敏感，存在精确率与召回率的权衡，且普遍低估罕见和长期副作用。结果表明，基于临床医生整理的副作用列表可显著提升LLM输出的可靠性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确传达癌症治疗的副作用对癌症幸存者的知情同意和后续护理至关重要，但因临床知识不足和电子健康记录系统分散而面临挑战。尽管大型语言模型（LLM）有潜力辅助该任务，其在肿瘤幸存者照护背景下的可靠性尚不明确。

Method: 构建21个乳腺癌患者档案，并设计仅在放疗方案上不同的配对临床场景，以评估七种指令调优的LLM在多种提示策略下的表现。将LLM生成的副作用列表与由多家医学中心七名以上乳腺放射肿瘤学家共同制定的临床专家参考标准进行比较。该参考标准依据放疗剂量-分割方式、照射区域和部位，按发生频率和时间 onset 对毒性进行分类。

Result: 发现不同LLM在生成副作用时对文档细微变化敏感，存在精确率与召回率之间的权衡，且系统性地遗漏罕见和长期副作用。单独使用数量限制会降低精确率，而将输出基于临床医生整理的副作用列表则显著提高可靠性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在肿瘤幸存者照护中自动生成放疗副作用列表仍存在重要局限，直接应用风险较高；建议采用临床专家知识约束和引导模型输出，以实现更安全、信息更充分的应用设计。

Abstract: Accurately communicating the side effects of cancer treatments to cancer survivors is critical, particularly in settings such as informed consent, where clinicians must clearly and comprehensively convey potential treatment toxicities. However, this task remains challenging due to clinical knowledge deficits about adverse treatment effects and fragmentation across electronic health record (EHR) systems. Large language models (LLMs) have the potential to assist in this task, though their reliability in oncology survivorship contexts remains poorly understood. We present a deployment-oriented stress-testing framework for evaluating LLM-generated radiation side effect lists in breast cancer treatment and survivorship care. Using 21 breast cancer patient profiles, we construct paired patient clinical scenarios that differ only in radiotherapy regimens to evaluate seven instruction-tuned LLMs under multiple prompting regimes. We then compare LLM outputs to a clinician-curated reference derived from informed consent documents at two major academic medical centers and developed by a team including more than seven breast radiation oncologists. The reference maps radiation dose-fractionation, fields, and locations to associated toxicities, broken down by frequency and temporal onset. Across models, we reveal sensitivity to minor documentation changes, trade-offs between precision and recall, and systematic under-recall of rare and long-term side effects. When used alone, constraints on the number of side effects generated reduce precision, and grounding outputs in clinician-curated side effect lists substantially improves reliability and robustness. These findings highlight important limitations of LLM use in oncology and suggest practical design choices for safer and more informative survivorship-focused applications.

</details>


### [41] [Revisiting the syntax of imperatives in Yemeni Arabic: An Agree across phases approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08447)
*Mohammed Q. Shormani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文采用“跨阶段一致”(AAP)方法分析也门阿拉伯语的祈使句句法，提出句法与话语之间的密切互动，解释了简单和复杂的祈使结构，包括A'-链结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨也门阿拉伯语中祈使句的句法结构及其与话语功能的接口，特别是信息结构与命题结构之间的联系。

Method: 采用跨阶段一致（AAP）理论框架，结合句法-话语接口分析，讨论祈使句中的主题、主语及A'-链的形成机制。

Result: 提出祈使句的题旨主语为第二人称pro，前置的代词或名词成分并非主语，而是关于话题（aboutness topic），并通过Match操作形成局部与非局部的A'-链；对于无显性话题的典型祈使句，提出存在一个隐性话题进入TopP的指示位置。

Conclusion: AAP方法能有效解释也门阿拉伯语祈使句的各种结构，揭示了句法与话语功能之间的深层联系。

Abstract: This article revisits the syntax of imperatives in Yemeni Arabic proposing an Agree acros phases (AAP) approach. I argue that the AAP approach successfully accounts for both simple and complex imperative constructions, including A'-chain structures, by establishing a close interactions between syntax and discourse. The study demonstrates that this interface is motivated by the interpretive and performative functions associated with imperatives, linking informational structure with propositional structure. It is also proposed that the thematic subject of imperatives is a 2-person pro, whereas any overt pronominal or nominal element occurring preverbally is not a subject, but rather a C-domain element, precisely aboutness topic. These topics serve as the logical subjects of imperatives and enter into a coreferentiality relationship with pro. This relation is analyzed as APP involving Match, yielding both local and non-local A'-chains. For core imperatives, viz., lacking an overt topic, I propose a null topic to (re)merge in Spec,TopP, whose interpretation depends on the discourse.

</details>


### [42] [Do Benchmarks Underestimate LLM Performance? Evaluating Hallucination Detection With LLM-First Human-Adjudicated Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08462)
*I. F. Atasoy,B. Mutlu,E. A. Sezer,A. Wahdan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在摘要任务中基于上下文的幻觉检测问题，通过对比人类标注与大模型（Gemini和GPT）的推理判断，发现原始人工标注存在不足；引入跨文化人工仲裁后，人机三方一致性及模型准确率均显著提升，表明模型辅助的再评估能生成更可靠的基准数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型在基于上下文的任务（如RAG和智能体系统）中因人工标注不一致导致的幻觉检测可靠性问题，提升评估基准的质量。

Method: 分析QAGS-C和SummEval数据集，比较Gemini 2.5 Flash和GPT-5 Mini的推理与原文跨度预测结果，并对人机判断分歧样本进行双人跨文化人工仲裁，重新评估标注一致性。

Result: 仲裁后，QAGS-C和SummEval上的人机三方一致率分别提高6.38%和7.62%；GPT模型准确率提升4.25%和2.34%，Gemini提升8.51%和3.80%；人工仲裁员间一致性为83%-87%，且常支持提供明确推理的模型判断。

Conclusion: 对于易产生歧义的任务，单次人工标注可能不足，结合模型推理与人工仲裁的再评估方法可建立更可靠、稳健的评估基准。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a persistent challenge in Large Language Models (LLMs), particularly in context-grounded settings such as RAG and agentic AI systems. This study focuses on contextual hallucination detection in summarization tasks. We analyze the QAGS-C and SummEval datasets by comparing original benchmark annotations with reason and span-based predictions from Gemini 2.5 Flash and GPT-5 Mini. To address systematic divergences between human labels and LLM judgments, we re-evaluated all conflicted samples through a human adjudication process involving 2 cross-cultural adjudicators. Following this re-evaluation, triple agreement (between human, GPT, and Gemini) increased by 6.38% for QAGS-C and 7.62% for SummEval. Similarly, model accuracy improved, with GPT increasing by 4.25% on QAGS-C and 2.34% on SummEval, while Gemini showed gains of 8.51% and 3.80%, respectively. Notably, adjudicators frequently sided with the models' judgments over original human annotations when LLMs provided explicit reasoning. Overall human adjudicator agreement ranged between 83% and 87%. These findings suggest that for ambiguity-prone tasks, single-pass annotations may be insufficient, and model-assisted re-evaluation yields more reliable benchmarks.

</details>


### [43] [PYTHALAB-MERA: Validation-Grounded Memory, Retrieval, and Acceptance Control for Frozen-LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08468)
*Mehmet Iscan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PYTHALAB-MERA是一种轻量级外部控制器，通过验证条件生成代码，结合冻结的本地语言模型与外部记忆和检索机制，在严格验证的强化学习编码任务中显著提升通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法如静态检索、长上下文提示、自我 refinement 和基于执行反馈的修复等无法同时提供基于验证的记忆、自适应检索-动作选择、延迟信用分配以及针对冻结本地模型的结构化技能复用。因此需要一种新框架来弥补这些不足。

Method: 提出PYTHALAB-MERA，使用冻结的语言模型生成完整源文件，由外部控制器管理记忆记录和AST衍生技能的选择，通过快速失败验证管道评估候选方案，将结果转化为有界奖励，并利用TD(lambda)式的资格迹进行延迟信用分配。

Result: 在三个强化学习编码任务、三次重复、每次最多三次尝试的设置下，PYTHALAB-MERA通过了8/9的严格验证，而自我 refinement 基线和GRACE扩展均未通过任何验证（0/9）。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持一个有限但明确的结论：在该设定下，引入外部记忆与检索控制机制显著提升了代码生成的验证成功率，但不意味着通用代码合成能力或形式化正确性已达成。

Abstract: Local LLM-based coding agents increasingly work in settings where correctness is earned through execution feedback, persistent state, and bounded repair, not through a single fluent answer. Static retrieval, long-context prompting, self-refinement, execution-feedback repair, and reinforcement learning over model weights each address part of this setting, but they do not jointly provide validation-grounded episodic memory, adaptive retrieval-action selection, delayed credit assignment, and structural skill reuse around a frozen local model. We introduce PYTHALAB-MERA, a lightweight external controller for local validation-conditioned code generation. The frozen language model proposes complete source files; the controller decides which memory records and AST-derived skills should enter the next prompt, validates each candidate through a fail-fast pipeline, converts validation outcomes into bounded shaped rewards, and propagates delayed credit through TD(lambda)-style eligibility traces. We evaluate the implementation as a local CLI artifact on reinforcement-learning coding tasks with strict validation gates. In the measured hard RL setting with three tasks, three repetitions, and a three-attempt budget, PYTHALAB-MERA passed 8/9 strict validations; the self-refinement baseline and the investigated GRACE extension each passed 0/9. These results support a deliberately bounded claim: in this recorded setting, the external memory-and-retrieval controller improved validation success. They do not establish general-purpose code synthesis, state-of-the-art performance, formal program correctness, or formal safety.

</details>


### [44] [A Computational Operationalisation of Competing Maturational Theories of Syntactic Development via Statistical Grammar Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08476)
*Mila Marcheva,Suchir Salhan,Weiwei Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过计算方法比较了儿童在语言发展过程中句法范畴习得的两种理论（自下而上和向内发展），发现自下而上的GROWING模型在三种评估指标上显著优于INWARD模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨儿童在第一语言发展过程中中间句法范畴的习得顺序，并比较不同成熟理论的预测能力。

Method: 使用统计语法归纳方法，对GROWING和INWARD两种理论假设进行计算建模，在保持输入和学习算法一致的前提下分析其可学习性。

Result: GROWING模型在三种评估指标上均显著优于INWARD模型，表明自下而上的句法结构发展路径更具解释力。

Conclusion: 研究支持自下而上的句法习得路径，表明儿童首先掌握词汇和屈折结构，之后逐步发展更高层次的句法范畴。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with what intermediate syntactic categories children acquire during first language development, and in what order. Maturational theories make different predictions. Bottom-up accounts (GROWING) propose that lexical and inflectional structure emerges first, while inward accounts (INWARD) predict early access to discourse-related categories. We computationally operationalise these hypotheses of staged syntactic emergence using statistical grammar induction, asking what each proposed ordering makes learnable when input and learning algorithm are held constant. Our framework makes category acquisition explicit and allows us to explore how different maturational orderings shape the structure that can be learned under identical conditions. Based on this operationalisation, the GROWING account significantly outperforms the INWARD account across three evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [45] [NARRA-Gym for Evaluating Interactive Narrative Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08503)
*Yue Huang,Yuchen Ma,Jiayi Ye,Wenjie Wang,Zipeng Ling,Xingjian Hu,Yuexing Hao,Zichen Chen,Zhangchen Xu,Yunhong He,Zhengqing Yuan,Yujun Zhou,Kehan Guo,Chaoran Chen,Toby Jia-Jun Li,Stefan Feuerriegel,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了NARRA-Gym，一个用于评估大语言模型在交互式叙事任务中表现的可执行环境，能够全面评测模型在故事生成、长期上下文管理、角色模拟和个性化等方面的综合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估方法多集中于静态提示或孤立的故事生成，缺乏对模型在多轮交互中维持连贯叙事、情感共鸣及用户自适应能力的系统性评估。因此需要构建更贴近实际应用场景的评测框架。

Method: 设计并实现NARRA-Gym，将稀疏的情感种子转化为完整的交互式故事片段，并记录模型在整个过程中的行为轨迹；通过LLM-as-judge的方法对8个基准人物进行控制实验，并结合人类评分进行评估。

Result: 在九个前沿大语言模型上的实验显示，不同模型在鲁棒性、用户体验和敏感个性化方面存在显著差异，部分生成流畅的模型在其他维度表现不佳。

Conclusion: 交互式叙事是一个有效的基准，可用于评估大语言模型在长时程、用户自适应场景下的综合行为表现，超越传统仅关注单次生成质量的评测方式。

Abstract: Interactive narrative tasks require LLMs to sustain a coherent, evolving story while adapting to a user over multiple turns. However, suitable benchmarks for this setting are limited: existing evaluations often focus on static prompts, isolated story generations, or post-hoc ratings, and therefore miss whether models can jointly manage story generation, long-context state and pacing, character simulation, empathic personalization, and story-grounded artifacts. We introduce NARRA-Gym, an executable evaluation environment that turns a sparse emotional seed into a complete interactive story episode and logs the full model-in-the-loop trajectory, including story construction, memory updates, planning, pacing interventions, and optional artifact synthesis. We evaluate nine frontier LLMs using a controlled LLM-as-judge sweep over eight benchmark personas and a human evaluation in which participants rate customized model outputs. Our results show substantial variation across models, personas, and evaluation dimensions: models that produce fluent stories can still fail on robustness, user experience, or resistance-sensitive personalization. These findings suggest that interactive narrative offers a useful benchmark for evaluating long-horizon, user-adaptive LLM behavior beyond isolated story quality.

</details>


### [46] [A Single Layer to Explain Them All:Understanding Massive Activations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08504)
*Zeru Shi,Zhenting Wang,Fan Yang,Qifan Wang,Ruixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型中大规模激活的起源，发现了一个名为“大规模涌现层（ME Layer）”的关键层，并提出了一种有效方法来减少其带来的表示刚性，从而提升模型在多种任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模激活在大语言模型中普遍存在，但其来源和影响尚不清晰，尤其是对隐藏状态多样性和注意力机制的限制，促使作者探究其成因并提出改进方法。

Method: 通过分析不同模型族中的激活模式，识别出ME Layer，并研究RMSNorm与FFN在此层中的作用；提出一种简单有效的方法，在不破坏性能的前提下减弱大规模激活带来的表示刚性。

Result: 所提方法在无需训练和微调两种设置下，均能持续提升大语言模型在指令遵循和数学推理等任务上的性能，并能缓解attention sinks问题。

Conclusion: ME Layer是大规模激活首次出现的关键层，其导致的表示不变性限制了模型表达能力，而新方法可有效缓解该问题，为理解并改进大语言模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: We investigate the origins of massive activations in large language models (LLMs) and identify a specific layer named the \textbf{Massive Emergence Layer (ME Layer)}, that is consistently observed across model families, where massive activations first emerge and subsequently propagate to deeper layers through residual connections. We show that, within the ME Layer both the RMSNorm and the FFN parameters jointly contribute to the emergence of massive activations. Once formed, the massive activation token representation remains largely invariant across layers, reducing the diversity of hidden representations passed to the attention module. Motivated by this limitation, we propose a simple and effective method to reduce the rigidity of the massive activation token. Our approach consistently improves LLM performance across multiple tasks, including instruction following and math reasoning, in both training free and fine tuning settings. Moreover, we show that our method mitigates attention sinks by selectively weakening their influence, elucidating their origin at the hidden state level and shedding new light on principled mitigation strategies.

</details>


### [47] [A Single Neuron Is Sufficient to Bypass Safety Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08513)
*Hamid Kazemi,Atoosa Chegini,Maria Safi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过定位语言模型中的拒绝神经元和概念神经元，研究发现安全对齐机制依赖于个别关键神经元，抑制这些神经元可导致安全机制失效，且该现象在多种模型中普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型安全对齐机制的鲁棒性及其内在神经机制，理解安全行为是否由特定神经元主导。

Method: 通过干预单个拒绝神经元和概念神经元，测试模型在有害请求和无害提示下的响应变化，验证其对安全对齐的影响。

Result: 在七个不同规模和架构的模型中，仅需抑制一个拒绝神经元即可绕过安全对齐；同时可通过放大概念神经元从无害提示生成有害内容。

Conclusion: 安全对齐并非广泛分布在模型权重中，而是由少数关键神经元决定，表明当前安全机制存在根本性的脆弱性。

Abstract: Safety alignment in language models operates through two mechanistically distinct systems: refusal neurons that gate whether harmful knowledge is expressed, and concept neurons that encode the harmful knowledge itself. By targeting a single neuron in each system, we demonstrate both directions of failure -- bypassing safety on explicit harmful requests via suppression, and inducing harmful content from innocent prompts via amplification -- across seven models spanning two families and 1.7B to 70B parameters, without any training or prompt engineering. Our findings suggest that safety alignment is not robustly distributed across model weights but is mediated by individual neurons that are each causally sufficient to gate refusal behavior -- suppressing any one of the identified refusal neurons bypasses safety alignment across diverse harmful requests.

</details>


### [48] [Coordinates of Capability: A Unified MTMM-Geometric Framework for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08522)
*Adib Sakhawat,Tahsin Islam,Takia Farhin,Syed Rifat Raiyan,Hasan Mahmud,Md Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多特质多方法（MTMM）的通用框架，通过几何流形视角统一九种大语言模型评估指标，将模型行为分解为不稳定性与敏感性、位置与对齐、覆盖与表达性三个正交潜在维度，提升评估的构念效度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型评估存在构念效度问题，碎片化基准和临时指标常混淆方法变异与真实能力，缺乏统一理论框架。

Method: 引入MTMM框架，将多种评估指标形式化为共享潜在空间中的几何测量，并在连续几何流形上进行统一建模。

Result: 成功统一了包括Paraphrase Instability、Drift Score等在内的九个评估指标，并将其映射到三个正交的潜在维度中，实现了对模型行为的解耦分析。

Conclusion: 该框架为大语言模型评估提供了理论严谨、领域无关的分类体系，有助于设计更稳健、经验上稳定的基准测试。

Abstract: The evaluation of Large Language Models (LLMs) faces a critical challenge in construct validity, where fragmented benchmarks and ad hoc metrics frequently conflate method variance, such as prompt sensitivity, with true latent capabilities. Concurrently, emerging research suggests that LLM capabilities and outputs can be modeled as continuous geometric manifolds. In this Systematization of Knowledge (SoK), we bridge these paradigms by proposing a generalized Multi-Trait Multi-Method (MTMM) framework for LLM evaluation. We formalize and unify nine evaluation metrics, including Paraphrase Instability, Drift Score, Overton Width, and Pluralism Score, interpreting them not as isolated scalar values but as geometric measurements within a shared latent coordinate space. This spatial unification factorizes model behavior into three orthogonal latent dimensions: (1) Instability and Sensitivity, (2) Position and Alignment, and (3) Coverage and Expressiveness. By systematically separating task-irrelevant perturbations from true capability spans, the framework provides a theoretically grounded and domain-agnostic taxonomy for robust and empirically stable benchmark design.

</details>


### [49] [100,000+ Movie Reviews from Kazakhstan: Russian, Kazakh, and Code-Switched Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08600)
*Rustem Yeshpanov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个包含100,502条来自哈萨克斯坦电影评论的新公开语料库，涵盖多种语言（主要是俄语、哈萨克语及语码转换文本），并进行了语言和情感极性标注，部分评论包含用户评分。研究设定了两类情感任务，并比较了传统模型与多语言Transformer模型的性能，结果显示后者在极性分类上表现更优，但评分分类仍因数据不平衡和评分间差异细微而具有挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个来自中亚地区的多语言电影评论数据集，以促进非主流语言和多语言环境下情感分析的研究。

Method: 收集并整理kino.kz网站2001-2025年间的电影评论，进行语言识别和情感极性人工标注；对含用户评分的子集开展五级评分分类任务；采用TF-IDF/BoW基线模型与mBERT、XLM-RoBERTa、RemBERT等多语言Transformer模型进行对比实验。

Result: Transformer模型在三类情感极性分类任务中显著优于传统模型；但在五类评分分类任务中表现不佳，主要受限于严重的类别不平衡和相邻评分等级之间的细微差别。

Conclusion: 多语言Transformer模型适用于哈萨克斯坦多语言环境下的情感分析，尤其在情感极性分类任务中表现良好，但复杂评分预测仍需进一步研究以解决数据分布和细粒度区分的问题。

Abstract: We present a new publicly available corpus of 100,502 movie reviews from Kazakhstan collected from kino.kz, spanning 2001-2025 and covering 4,943 unique titles. The dataset is multilingual, consisting mainly of Russian reviews alongside Kazakh and code-switched texts. Reviews are manually annotated for language and sentiment polarity, and 11,309 reviews additionally contain explicit user-provided ratings. We define two sentiment tasks -- three-way polarity classification and five-class score classification -- and benchmark classical BoW/TF-IDF baselines against multilingual transformer models (mBERT, XLM-RoBERTa, RemBERT). Experimental results show that transformer models consistently outperform classical baselines on polarity classification, while score classification remains challenging under leakage-controlled evaluation due to severe class imbalance and subtle distinctions between adjacent rating levels.

</details>


### [50] [PARD-2: Target-Aligned Parallel Draft Model for Dual-Mode Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08632)
*Zihao An,Taichi Liu,Ziqiong Liu,Dong Li,Ruofeng Liu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了PARD-2，一种双模式推测解码框架，通过自适应重加权令牌优化（CAT）提升大语言模型推理速度，在多种模型和任务上实现了最高达6.94倍的无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的草稿模型训练目标未与推理时最大化连续令牌接受长度的目标对齐，导致推测解码效率受限。

Method: 基于PARD框架，提出PARD-2，引入置信度自适应令牌（CAT）优化方法，并构建支持目标依赖与目标无关两种模式的统一草稿模型。

Result: 在Llama3.1-8B等模型上，PARD-2相比EAGLE-3提升1.9倍，相比PARD提升1.3倍，实现最高6.94倍的无损加速。

Conclusion: PARD-2通过重新设计训练目标以匹配推理目标，显著提升了推测解码的效率，为大语言模型的高效推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates Large Language Models (LLMs) inference by using a lightweight draft model to propose candidate tokens that are verified in parallel by the target model. However, existing draft model training objectives are not directly aligned with the inference-time goal of maximizing consecutive token acceptance. To address this issue, we reformulate the draft model optimization objective, shifting the focus from token prediction accuracy to the overall acceptance length. In this paper, we build upon PARD to propose PARD-2, a dual-mode speculative decoding framework with Confidence-Adaptive Token (CAT) optimization. This approach adaptively reweights each token to better align with the verification process. Notably, PARD-2 enables a single draft model to support both target-dependent and target-independent modes. Experiments across diverse models and tasks demonstrate that PARD-2 achieves up to 6.94$\times$ lossless acceleration, surpassing EAGLE-3 by 1.9$\times$ and PARD by 1.3$\times$ on Llama3.1-8B. Our code is available at https://github.com/AMD-AGI/PARD.

</details>


### [51] [EdgeFlowerTune: Evaluating Federated LLM Fine-Tuning Under Realistic Edge System Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08636)
*Jiaxiang Geng,Yiyi Lu,Lunyu Zhao,Yan Gao,Nicholas D. Lane,Bing Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EdgeFlowerTune是一个面向真实边缘设备的联邦大语言模型微调基准平台，联合评估模型质量与系统开销，揭示仅关注准确率可能误导部署决策，支持系统感知的可重复评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦微调研究多集中于跨机构或模拟环境，忽视真实边缘设备的资源与运行时限制，难以反映方法在实际部署中的可行性。

Method: 提出EdgeFlowerTune，构建基于Flower和MobileFineTuner的真实设备平台，涵盖商用安卓手机和NVIDIA边缘开发板；设计三类评估协议（Quality-under-Budget、Cost-to-Target、Robustness），联合衡量模型质量与通信、延迟、内存、能耗及动态环境适应性等系统成本。

Result: 实验表明，在相似最终模型质量下，不同方法在通信开销、运行时间、内存占用和能耗等方面差异显著；仅以准确率评价会忽略这些关键差异，导致错误的部署决策。

Conclusion: EdgeFlowerTune为边缘侧联邦大模型微调提供了可复现、系统感知的评估基准，强调必须联合考虑模型性能与系统约束以实现真正可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning offers a promising paradigm for adapting large language models (LLMs) on edge devices by leveraging the rich, diverse, and continuously generated data from smartphones and IoT devices without compromising user data privacy. Such edge-side adaptation can improve model personalization, robustness, and responsiveness to local contexts. However, the practical feasibility of federated LLM fine-tuning on real edge devices remains unclear, as most existing work focuses on cross-silo or simulation-based settings, overlooking the resource and runtime constraints that determine whether a method is deployable on real edge systems. We present EdgeFlowerTune, a deployment-oriented benchmark for federated LLM fine-tuning under realistic edge-system constraints. EdgeFlowerTune jointly evaluates model quality and system costs, including communication, wall-clock latency, memory usage, energy consumption, and robustness to dynamic edge conditions. To compare methods in terms of effectiveness, efficiency, and robustness, EdgeFlowerTune introduces three complementary protocols: Quality-under-Budget, Cost-to-Target, and Robustness. We instantiate EdgeFlowerTune as a real-device platform built on Flower and MobileFineTuner, spanning commercial Android smartphones and NVIDIA edge development boards. Our benchmark results show that accuracy-only evaluation can lead to misleading conclusions: methods with similar final quality may differ substantially in deployability once realistic system constraints are considered. EdgeFlowerTune provides a reproducible benchmark for system-aware evaluation of federated LLM fine-tuning at the edge.

</details>


### [52] [AgentCollabBench: Diagnosing When Good Agents Make Bad Collaborators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08647)
*Aritra Mazumder,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Nusrat Jahan Lia,Tanzila Khan,Kainat Raisa Hossain,Nehaa Shri,Shubhrangshu Debsarkar,Humayra Tasnim,Gour Gupal Talukder Shawon,Debjoty Mitra,Sumaiya Ahmed Rani,Al Jami Islam Anik,Al Nafeu Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgentCollabBench，一个用于诊断多智能体系统中多跳推理过程漏洞的基准测试，揭示了现有基于结果的评估方法无法发现的协作风险，并指出系统结构设计对可靠性至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体系统评估主要依赖最终输出的正确性，难以发现推理过程中因信息传递失真导致的隐蔽错误，因此需要一种能够衡量协作过程中结构性脆弱性的诊断工具。

Method: 构建了一个包含900个人工验证任务的基准AgentCollabBench，覆盖软件工程、DevOps和数据工程领域，针对四种行为风险（指令衰减、虚假信念传播、上下文泄漏和追踪数据耐久性）进行测试，并在四种主流大模型上评估不同通信拓扑对信息传递完整性的影响。

Result: 发现了不同模型在各类风险中的表现差异，例如Qwen-3.5-35B-A3B在指令稳定性和追踪耐久性上表现更好，而GPT 4.1 mini在防止信息泄漏和虚假信念传播方面更优；同时发现通信拓扑结构是影响信息存活率的关键因素，可解释7%-40%的方差，尤其是汇聚型DAG结构中的合成瓶颈会导致少数路径的信息被丢弃。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统的可靠性不仅取决于单个模型的能力，更受系统架构特别是通信拓扑结构的显著影响，仅提升模型智能无法弥补结构缺陷，必须从系统设计层面优化信息流动路径。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems achieve state-of-the-art outcomes through peer collaboration. However, when an agent in the pipeline silently drops a constraint, the system's final output may look correct even though the reasoning chain was quietly corrupted, and existing outcome-based evaluations are blind to such multi-hop process failures. To make these vulnerabilities measurable before deployment, we introduce AgentCollabBench, a diagnostic benchmark of 900 human-validated tasks spanning software engineering, DevOps, and data engineering. Each task isolates one of four behavioral risks: instruction decay (does a constraint survive peer pressure?), false-belief contagion (does a falsehood spread through consensus?), context leakage (does information bleed between tasks?), and tracer durability (does marked data reach the final agent?). Evaluating four modern LLMs (GPT 4.1 mini, Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite, Qwen-3.5-35B-A3B, and Llama 3.1 8B Instruct), we expose model-specific vulnerability profiles invisible to outcome-only evaluation; Qwen-3.5-35B-A3B, for example, leads on tracer durability and instruction stability, while GPT 4.1 mini leads on leakage containment and false-belief resistance. Beyond per-model differences, communication topology emerges as a primary risk factor that explains 7-40% of the variance in multi-hop information survival. The effect traces to a synthesis bottleneck specific to converging-DAG nodes: an agent weighing competing parent inputs discards constraints carried by a minority branch, a bottleneck structurally absent from linear chains. AgentCollabBench demonstrates that suboptimal topology can silently erase the safeguards of highly capable models, arguing that multi-agent reliability is fundamentally a structural problem and that scaling model intelligence alone is no substitute for architecture.

</details>


### [53] [Hint Tuning: Less Data Makes Better Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08665)
*Siqi Fan,Minghao Li,Xiaoqian Ma,Xiusheng Huang,Zhuo Chen,Bowen Qin,Liujie Zhang,Shuo Shang,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Hint Tuning方法，通过指令模型自动探测问题难度并构建三类提示状态（无提示、稀疏提示、全提示），仅用1K样本实现主流推理模型24-66%的token减少，同时保持准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型在所有问题上均使用冗长的思维链推理，导致生成过多不必要的token，且未根据问题难度调整推理深度，造成资源浪费。

Method: 利用对应的指令模型作为难度探测器，通过测试其在不同引导下的解题能力，自动生成包含No-Hint、Sparse-Hint和Full-Hint三种状态的训练数据，并以此微调推理模型以适应不同难度的问题。

Result: 在多个主流推理模型（如Qwen3-Thinking、DeepSeek-R1-Distill）和不同规模（4B–32B）下，仅使用1K自标注样本即可实现24%-66%的token减少（平均31.5%），并在五个基准上保持竞争力的准确性。

Conclusion: Hint Tuning提供了一种数据高效、低成本的方法来校准推理深度，无需大量蒸馏数据或昂贵的强化学习，通过与指令模型能力对齐即可显著提升推理效率。

Abstract: Large reasoning models achieve high accuracy through extended chain-of-thought but generate 5--8 more tokens than necessary, applying verbose reasoning uniformly regardless of problem difficulty. We propose Hint Tuning, a data-efficient approach that teaches models to calibrate reasoning depth. Our key insight: the corresponding instruct model serves as an ideal difficulty probe. By testing what the instruct model can solve with varying guidance, we automatically construct training data across three states: No-Hint (direct answer), Sparse-Hint (minimal prefix), and Full-Hint (complete reasoning). This converts the abstract challenge of difficulty labeling into a measurable consistency check between the instruct and reasoning models. With only 1K self-annotated samples, Hint Tuning achieves 24--66% token reduction (31.5% average) across mainstream reasoning models (Qwen3-Thinking, DeepSeek-R1-Distill) at multiple scales (4B--32B) while maintaining competitive accuracy on five benchmarks. Unlike methods requiring massive distillation datasets or expensive RL, we achieve superior efficiency through simple alignment with the instruct model's capabilities.

</details>


### [54] [Explanation Fairness in Large Language Models: An Empirical Analysis of Disparities in How LLMs Justify Decisions Across Demographic Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08671)
*Gautam Veldanda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个解释公平性分类法（EFT），用于评估大语言模型在不同人口群体中生成决策解释的公平性，并通过实证研究揭示了多个维度上的显著差异，表明模型选择和提示策略对解释公平性具有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI决策公平性已被广泛研究，但AI解释的公平性（即LLM是否在不同人群间以相同质量、深度、语气和语言复杂度进行解释）尚未受到足够关注。

Method: 提出了解释公平性分类法（EFT），包含五个可操作的维度，并在80个提示模板、四个关键决策领域和五个主流LLM上进行了实证研究；引入了两种新的黑箱指标：Hedging Density Score（HDS）和Explanation Faithfulness Proxy（EFP）。

Result: 在最多400个提示对上的实验显示，所有八个EFT指标均存在统计显著差异（Cohen's d从小到大，所有p_BH < 10^(-62)）；Qwen3 32B的 verbosity 差异是LLaMA 3.3 70B的5.9倍；两种基于提示的缓解方法使EFP差异减少了78-95%，但对风格维度无效。

Conclusion: 解释不公平现象广泛存在于当前LLM中，且与模型选择密切相关；风格类不公平根植于预训练数据分布，仅靠部署阶段的提示指令难以消除，需建立可复现的解释公平性评估框架以支持AI监管与实践。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed not only to make decisions but to explain them. While AI decision fairness has been studied extensively, the fairness of AI explanations (whether LLMs justify decisions with equal quality, depth, tone, and linguistic sophistication across demographic groups) has received little attention. This paper introduces the Explanation Fairness Taxonomy (EFT), a framework comprising five formally defined, operationalizable dimensions: Verbosity Disparity, Sentiment Disparity, Epistemic Hedging Disparity, Decision-Linked Explanation Disparity, and Lexical Complexity Disparity. The taxonomy is instantiated in a controlled empirical study across 80 prompt templates, four consequential decision domains (hiring, medical triage, credit assessment, legal judgment), and five LLMs: GPT-4.1, Claude Sonnet, LLaMA 3.3 70B, GPT-OSS 120B, and Qwen3 32B. Two novel black-box metrics are introduced: the Hedging Density Score (HDS) and the Explanation Faithfulness Proxy (EFP), a heuristic indicator of decision-linked explanation variation. Across up to 400 prompt pairs, all eight EFT metrics show statistically significant disparities (Cohen's d ranging from small to large, all p_BH < 10^(-62)). Model choice is strongly associated with disparity magnitude: Qwen3 32B exhibits verbosity disparities 5.9x larger than LLaMA 3.3 70B. Two prompting-based mitigations show significant reductions in EFP disparity (78-95%) but no significant effect on stylistic dimensions, consistent with the hypothesis that stylistic explanation inequalities are encoded in pre-training distributions and are not resolvable through deployment-level instruction alone. A reproducible measurement framework is offered for explanation-level fairness auditing, with implications for AI regulation and deployment practice.

</details>


### [55] [Breaking the Impasse: Dual-Scale Evolutionary Policy Training for Social Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08721)
*Minzheng Wang,Run Luo,Yanbo Wang,Zichen Liu,Yuqiao Tan,Tao Tan,Xu Nan,Yinhe Zheng,Wenji Mao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出双尺度进化策略训练（DEPT）方法，解决强化学习在开放性语言社交游戏中因策略同质化导致的进化停滞问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放性语言社交游戏中，传统基于可验证奖励的强化学习容易因策略空间过大而陷入同质化，导致进化梯度消失。

Method: 设计时间尺度感知机制，通过量化双尺度价值基线差异和对局熵检测进化停滞，并引入非对称优势重塑动态调整优化 landscape。

Result: 在多个社交语言游戏中验证了DEPT的有效性，相比强基线能避免策略退化并持续推动策略演化。

Conclusion: DEPT能够有效恢复梯度信号，促进语言智能体在复杂社交环境中的长期策略探索与进化。

Abstract: While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has proven effective for closed-ended tasks, extending it to open-ended social language games via self-play reveals a critical issue: evolution impasse. Due to the vast strategy space, language agents frequently converge to homogenized behaviors, leading to deterministic match outcomes that eliminate the gradient signals necessary for policy evolution. To tackle this issue, we propose Dual-scale Evolutionary Policy Training (DEPT) for social language games. DEPT introduces a time-scaled evolutionary perception mechanism that detects impasse by quantifying dual-scale value baseline divergence alongside match entropy. Upon perceiving the collapse, it then activates asymmetric advantage reshaping to dynamically modulate the optimization landscape for intervention. Thus, our method effectively restores gradient signals and enforces sustained strategic exploration. Extensive experiments on multiple social language games demonstrate that DEPT outperforms strong baselines, avoiding policy degeneration and driving the continuous evolution of social language agents.

</details>


### [56] [Training with Harnesses: On-Policy Harness Self-Distillation for Complex Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08741)
*Zhengyang Zhao,Lu Ma,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了On-Policy Harness Self-Distillation (OPHSD)，利用推理时增强机制作为教师模型，通过自蒸馏将任务特定能力内化到学生模型中，从而提升大语言模型在复杂推理任务上的泛化性和独立性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时增强方法虽然能提升模型表现，但并未改变模型本身的内在能力，因此需要一种方法将这些外部增强的能力整合进模型内部。

Method: 提出OPHSD方法，使用当前模型结合推理时增强机制作为教师模型，对未增强的学生模型进行自蒸馏训练，从而将外部Harness提供的额外监督信号融入模型参数中。

Result: 在文本分类的draft-verify和数学推理的plan-solve等任务上，OPHSD显著优于强基线方法（如在HMMT25上比OPSD高出10.83%），且分析表明推理时重新启用Harness不再带来增益甚至可能降低性能。

Conclusion: 推理时的复杂Harness可作为临时训练支架，其优势可通过OPHSD永久反馈回基础模型，无需在推理阶段持续依赖。

Abstract: Inference-time harnesses substantially improve large language models on complex reasoning tasks. However, the intrinsic capabilities of the underlying model remain unchanged by the addition of these external workflows. To bridge this gap, we introduce \emph{On-Policy Harness Self-Distillation} (OPHSD), which employs the harness-augmented current model as a teacher for self-distillation, thereby introducing extra supervisory signals from the harness beyond training data. OPHSD internalizes task-specific harness capabilities into the student model, yielding robust generalizability and strong standalone performance across diverse reasoning tasks. Evaluated across draft--verify harness for text classification and plan--solve for mathematical reasoning tasks, OPHSD consistently outperforms strong baselines (e.g., +10.83\% over OPSD on HMMT25). Our analysis further indicates that reattaching the harness during inference yields no additional benefits and can even degrade performance, suggesting that complex harnesses need not always be permanent fixtures; instead, they can serve as temporary training scaffolds whose benefits are permanently fed back into the base model. Our code and training data are available at https://github.com/zzy1127/OPHSD-On-Policy-Harness-Self-Distillation.

</details>


### [57] [Narrative Landscape: Mapping Narrative Dispositions Across LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08742)
*Donghoon Jung,Jiwoo Choi,Songeun Chae,Seohyon Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种量化分析大语言模型（LLM）行为倾向的框架，通过受控任务测量模型在重复实验中的一致性与多样性，并利用主成分分析可视化不同模型的选择模式，揭示了模型家族间的刚性-探索谱系及指令类型对选择空间结构的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了系统理解大语言模型在不同条件下的稳定输出特征，需要一种可量化的框架来刻画其内在的行为倾向，而非仅依赖任务性能指标。

Method: 设计了一个结构化的叙事约束选择任务，在六个前沿大模型和三种指令类型上进行多次测试，使用Jaccard相似性衡量跨重复选择的一致性（consistency），用逆辛普森指数衡量选项分散度（diversity），并引入基于PCA的‘叙事景观’（Narrative Landscape）可视化方法进行模型间比较。

Result: 发现了模型家族之间存在明显的刚性-探索连续谱；尽管某些模型在标量指标上表现相似，但不同指令类型会改变其选择空间的几何结构，表明相同分数可能掩盖本质不同的选择模式。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效揭示大语言模型在行为倾向上的深层差异，强调在评估模型时应超越单一指标，关注其响应模式的结构特性。

Abstract: This study proposes a quantitative framework for profiling LLM dispositions as stable, model-specific regularities in output under repeated, controlled elicitation. Using a structured narrative constraint-selection task administered across six frontier models and three instruction types, we operationalize disposition through two dimensions: "consistency", measured as cross-replication selection overlap via Jaccard similarity, and "diversity", measured as dispersion across options via the inverse Simpson index. We further introduce Narrative Landscape, a PCA-based visualization that maps each model's selection profile into a shared space for direct comparison. Results reveal a clear rigidity-exploration spectrum across model families and show that instruction types shift the geometry of selection spaces even when scalar metrics appear similar, indicating that comparable scores can mask qualitatively distinct selection topologies.

</details>


### [58] [SimReg: Achieving Higher Performance in the Pretraining via Embedding Similarity Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08809)
*Yan Sun,Guoxia Wang,Jinle Zeng,JiaBin Yang,Shuai Li,Li Shen,Dacheng Tao,DianHai Yu,Haifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SimReg的嵌入相似性正则化损失方法，用于提升大语言模型预训练中的表示学习效率。该方法通过鼓励相同标签的词元表示更加相似，并利用对比损失将不同标签的词元分离开来，从而扩大多分类间隔，加速训练收敛并提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于基于下一个词元预测的预训练导致词元嵌入具有高类内方差和类间相似性，限制了表示学习的效率，因此需要探索在大规模预训练中有效的正则化方法。

Method: 提出了SimReg方法，结合相似性正则化与对比损失，在序列中使相同真实标签的词元表示更相似，不同标签的则相互分离。

Result: 实验表明，SimReg在密集和MoE架构上均能将训练收敛速度提高30%以上，并在多个标准基准上的平均零样本下游任务性能提升超过1%。

Conclusion: SimReg有效提升了大语言模型预训练的表示学习效率，显著加快收敛并增强下游任务表现，为未来预训练机制设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Pretraining large language models (LLMs) with next-token prediction has led to remarkable advances, yet the context-dependent nature of token embeddings in such models results in high intra-class variance and inter-class similarity, thus hindering the efficiency of representation learning. While similarity-based regularization has demonstrated benefit in supervised fine-tuning and classification tasks, its application and efficacy in large-scale LLM pretraining remains underexplored. In this work, we propose the SimReg, an embedding similarity regularization loss that explicitly encourages token representations with the same ground-truth label within each sequence to be more similar, while enforcing separation from different-label tokens via a contrastive loss. Our analysis reveals that this mechanism introduces gains by enlarging multi-classification margins, thereby enabling more efficient classification. Extensive experiments across dense and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures demonstrate that SimReg consistently accelerates training convergence by over 30% and improves average zero-shot downstream performance by over 1% across standard benchmarks. Further ablation studies and analyses offer practical insights into hyperparameter tuning and loss effectiveness.

</details>


### [59] [Generating Leakage-Free Benchmarks for Robust RAG Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08838)
*Jiayi Liu,Jiaxing Zhang,Bowen Jin,Jennifer Neville*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SeedRG的半合成基准生成管道，旨在缓解检索增强生成（RAG）系统评估中的知识泄露和基准老化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG基准数据集中存在知识泄露问题，许多问题可直接由大语言模型的参数记忆回答，导致评估不可靠，且随着基准重复使用，该问题因模型吸收其内容而加剧。

Method: 从种子基准数据集出发，SeedRG提取问题-上下文对的推理图以捕捉其推理结构，并通过类型约束的实体替换生成新样本；同时引入推理图一致性检查和知识泄露过滤两个验证步骤确保生成质量。

Result: SeedRG生成的实例在保持原始推理模式的同时具有新颖性，不易存在于模型参数知识中，从而有效降低知识泄露风险并延缓基准老化。

Conclusion: SeedRG能够生成结构合理、难度可控且抗知识泄露的RAG评估基准，为更可靠地评估检索性能提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is widely used to augment large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge. However, many benchmark datasets, designed to test RAG performance, comprise many questions that can already be answered from an LLM's parametric memory. This leads to unreliable evaluation. We refer to this phenomenon as knowledge leakage: cases where RAG tasks are solvable without retrieval. This issue worsens over time due to benchmark aging. As benchmarks are reused for training, their contents are increasingly absorbed into model parameters, making them less effective for evaluating retrieval.
  We introduce SeedRG, a semi-synthetic benchmark generation pipeline that mitigates knowledge leakage and addresses the issue of benchmark aging. Starting from a seed benchmark dataset, SeedRG extracts a reasoning graph from question-context pairs to capture their underlying reasoning structure, and then generates new examples via type-constrained entity replacement. This process produces structurally similar but novel instances that are unlikely to exist in the model's parametric knowledge, while preserving the original reasoning patterns. To ensure quality, we incorporate two verification steps: (1) a reasoning-graph consistency check to maintain task difficulty, and (2) a knowledge-leakage filter to exclude instances answerable without retrieval.

</details>


### [60] [ReST-KV: Robust KV Cache Eviction with Layer-wise Output Reconstruction and Spatial-Temporal Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08840)
*Yongqi An,Chang Lu,Kuan Zhu,Tao Yu,Chaoyang Zhao,Hong Wu,Ming Tang,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ReST-KV的新型KV缓存驱逐方法，通过层间输出重构和时空平滑机制，有效减少大语言模型在长序列生成中的解码延迟，并显著提升长上下文任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着序列长度增加，大语言模型中Key-Value缓存的内存需求不断上升，现有驱逐方法多依赖注意力权重，忽视了token移除导致的注意力再分配效应以及时空动态特性，因此需要更鲁棒的驱逐策略。

Method: 将KV缓存驱逐建模为最小化输出差异的优化问题，采用高效的层间重建来直接评估每个token移除对模型输出的影响；引入指数移动平均平滑处理时间变化，并设计自适应窗口机制捕捉空间模式。

Result: 在LongBench和RULER基准上分别超越现有最优方法2.58%和15.2%，在Needle-in-a-Haystack和InfiniteBench上也表现更优，且在128k上下文长度下实现高达10.61倍的解码延迟降低。

Conclusion: ReST-KV通过综合考虑注意力再分配和时空动态，提供了一种更全面、高效的KV缓存驱逐方案，显著提升了长上下文场景下的推理效率与模型表现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) face growing challenges in efficient generative inference due to the increasing memory demands of Key-Value (KV) caches, especially for long sequences. Existing eviction methods typically retain KV pairs with high attention weights but overlook the impact of attention redistribution caused by token removal, as well as the spatial-temporal dynamics in KV selection. In this paper, we propose ReST-KV, a robust KV eviction method that combines layer-wise output Reconstruction and Spatial-Temporal smoothing to provide a more comprehensive perspective for the KV cache eviction task. Specifically, ReST-KV formulates KV cache eviction as an optimization problem that minimizes output discrepancies through efficient layer-wise reconstruction. By directly modeling how each token's removal affects the model output, our method naturally captures attention redistribution effects, going beyond simplistic reliance on raw attention weights. To further enhance robustness, we design exponential moving average smoothing to handle temporal variations and an adaptive window-based mechanism to capture spatial patterns. Our method, ReST-KV, significantly advances performance on long-context benchmarks. It surpasses state-of-the-art baselines by 2.58% on LongBench and 15.2% on RULER. Additionally, ReST-KV consistently outperforms existing methods on Needle-in-a-Haystack and InfiniteBench, all while achieving a remarkable 10.61$\times$ reduction in decoding latency at 128k context length. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/an-yongqi/rest-kv to facilitate reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [61] [XPERT: Expert Knowledge Transfer for Effective Training of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08842)
*Chang Liu,Boyu Shi,Xu Yang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为XPERT的框架，通过分析MoE大语言模型中的专家激活模式，提取并重用跨领域的专家知识，以提升不同规模语言模型的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现部分专家在多个知识领域中均被激活，这些专家编码了可泛化的通用知识，启发作者探索如何有效复用此类可识别的专家知识。

Method: 通过推理阶段的分析识别跨领域专家，利用张量分解优化其表示，并将提取的知识迁移到下游模型中进行复用。

Result: 在语言理解与对话生成任务上，使用XPERT复用专家知识的模型表现出更优性能和更快收敛速度。

Conclusion: MoE大语言模型可作为结构化且可重用的知识源，专家级知识复用有助于提升模型训练效率与性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models organize knowledge into explicitly routed expert modules, making expert-level representations traceable and analyzable. By analyzing expert activation patterns in MoE large language models (LLMs), we find that a subset of experts is consistently activated across diverse knowledge domains. These common experts encode cross-domain, generalizable knowledge that is closely related to model generalization, naturally raising the question of how such identifiable expert knowledge can be practically reused. Motivated by this observation, we propose XPERT, a framework that extracts, consolidates, and reuses expert knowledge from pre-trained MoE LLMs to support more effective training of language models across different model scales. XPERT identifies cross-domain experts via inference-only analysis, refines their representations through tensor decomposition, and adapts the extracted knowledge to reuse in downstream models. Experiments on language understanding and dialogue generation benchmarks show that models benefiting from reused expert knowledge achieve consistently stronger performance and faster convergence compared to strong baselines. These results highlight MoE LLMs as structured and reusable knowledge sources, and demonstrate the value of expert-level knowledge reuse for improving model training.

</details>


### [62] [EmoS: A High-Fidelity Multimodal Benchmark for Fine-grained Streaming Emotional Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08847)
*Pengze Guo,Jingxi Liang,Zhiwen Xie,Qifeng Wang,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了EmoS，一个高保真的双语情感基准数据集，通过结合静态筛选片段和动态独白子集，解决了现有数据集在生态效度和噪声方面的局限性，并通过严格的双层人工标注确保了连续情感演化的准确标注。实验表明，在EmoS上微调多模态大语言模型可显著优于零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感识别基准在生态效度、信号清晰度和细粒度标注的可靠性方面存在不足，难以满足高压力、老龄化社会对共情支持模型的需求。

Method: 构建了一个包含严格筛选的静态片段和动态流式独白的双语情感数据集EmoS，并采用双层人工标注流程以确保标注质量，从而捕捉连续的情感变化。

Result: 在EmoS上微调多模态大语言模型后，性能显著优于零样本基线，验证了该数据集在训练和评估情感识别与共情模型中的有效性。

Conclusion: EmoS为未来情感识别和共情模型的训练与评估提供了高质量、高生态效度的基准，推动了相关领域的发展。

Abstract: In the context of today's high-pressure, aging society, the demand for large-scale emotional models capable of providing empathetic support is more critical than ever. However, existing benchmarks fail to simultaneously achieve ecological validity, signal clarity, and reliable fine-grained labeling. We introduce EmoS, a high-fidelity bilingual benchmark designed to resolve the limitations of ecological validity and noise in existing datasets by combining strictly filtered static slices with a dynamic Streaming Monologue subset. Supported by a rigorous dual-layer human annotation pipeline, EmoS provides trusted ground truth that captures continuous emotional evolution. Empirical results show that fine-tuning MLLMs (multimodal large language models) on EmoS yields significant gains over zero-shot baselines, laying the foundation for the training and evaluation of future emotion recognition models and empathy models. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/NLP2CT/EmoS.

</details>


### [63] [Architecture, Not Scale: Circuit Localization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08853)
*Sohan Venkatesh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，注意力架构对电路分析的复杂性影响大于模型参数量，分组查询注意力使电路更集中且机制更稳定，特定架构下的事实回忆电路在达到临界规模后会出现相变，表明可解释性难度并非仅由模型大小决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战随着模型规模扩大电路分析必然变得更难的假设，探索架构选择对可解释性的影响。

Method: 在Pythia和Qwen2.5模型中研究三种电路类型（间接对象识别、归纳头和事实回忆），比较标准多头注意力与分组查询注意力下的电路特性。

Result: 发现分组查询注意力产生的电路比标准多头注意力更集中且机制更稳定；在Qwen2.5系列中，事实回忆电路在超过某一临界规模时发生突变，形成单一瓶颈而非逐渐退化。

Conclusion: 某些架构选择（如分组查询注意力）可显著提升大模型的可解释性，说明模型可解释性的难度不仅取决于规模，更受架构影响。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability assumes that circuit analysis becomes harder as models scale. We challenge this assumption by showing that the attention architecture matters more than parameter count. Studying three circuit types across Pythia and Qwen2.5, we find that grouped query attention produces circuits that are far more concentrated and mechanistically stable than standard multi-head attention at comparable scales. The same concentration pattern holds across indirect object identification, induction heads, and factual recall. Within a single architecture family (Qwen2.5), factual recall circuits undergo a discrete phase transition above a critical scale, collapsing to a single bottleneck rather than degrading gradually. These findings suggest that some architectural choices make large models more tractable to study and that interpretability difficulty is not a fixed consequence of model size.

</details>


### [64] [Max-pooling Network Revisited: Analyzing the Role of Semantic Probability in Multiple Instance Learning for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08863)
*Shota Fujikawa,Issei Sato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需语义一致性计算的高效幻觉检测方法，通过最大化池化聚合词级特征并使用轻量级MLP直接估计句子得分，在保持竞争力性能的同时显著提升了效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合方法如HaMI虽然性能优越，但因重复采样和高成本的语义相似性计算导致计算开销大，因此需要一种更高效的幻觉检测方法。

Method: 从决策边距扩大的理论视角出发，重新审视经典句子分类模型，采用最大池化聚合token-level特征，并用轻量级MLP直接估计句子得分，实现内部特征表示的自适应聚合。

Result: 所提方法在不需要语义一致性计算的情况下，显著提高了计算效率，同时在多个基准上保持了与最先进方法相媲美的性能。

Conclusion: 通过边距扩大视角设计的轻量级模型可在不依赖昂贵语义计算的前提下，实现高效且有效的幻觉检测，为实际应用提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Hallucination detection has become increasingly important for improving the reliability of large language models (LLMs). Recently, hybrid approaches such as HaMI, which combine semantic consistency with internal model states via Multiple Instance Learning (MIL), have achieved state-of-the-art performance. However, these methods incur substantial computational overhead due to repeated sampling and costly semantic similarity computations. In this work, we first provide a theoretical analysis of HaMI in terms of decision margins, revealing that scaling internal states with semantic consistency leads to an enlarged decision margin. Motivated by this insight, we revisit classical sentence classification models from a margin enlargement perspective, aggregating token-level features via max pooling and directly estimating sentence scores using a lightweight MLP. Without requiring semantic consistency computations, our approach achieves substantial efficiency improvements while maintaining competitive performance with state-of-the-art baselines through adaptive aggregation of internal feature representations.

</details>


### [65] [DocScope: Benchmarking Verifiable Reasoning for Trustworthy Long-Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08888)
*Xiang Feng,Jiawei Zhou,Zhangfeng Huang,Kewei Wang,Shanshan Ye,Jinxin Hu,Zulong Chen,Yong Luo,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DocScope是一个新的基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在长篇、视觉丰富的文档上的推理能力，通过结构化推理轨迹预测和四阶段评估协议，揭示了现有模型在证据链完整性和区域定位方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评估方法主要关注端到端的答案准确性，无法全面衡量模型在长文档上生成可信、可验证推理的能力，因此需要一种更细粒度的评估框架。

Method: 提出DocScope基准，将长文档问答建模为结构化推理轨迹预测问题，并设计四阶段评估协议（页面定位、区域 grounding、事实提取和答案验证），各阶段独立审计，所有标注由人工完成。

Result: 实验表明，即使答案正确，完整的证据链率最高仅为29%，区域 grounding 是最薄弱环节；跨架构比较显示激活参数量比总规模更重要。

Conclusion: 答案准确率不能替代轨迹级评估，当前模型在整合远距离分散证据方面存在挑战，忠实感知和事实提取是主要瓶颈，激活参数量对性能影响更大。

Abstract: Evaluating whether Multimodal Large Language Models can produce trustworthy, verifiable reasoning over long, visually rich documents requires evaluation beyond end-to-end answer accuracy. We introduce DocScope, a benchmark that formulates long-document QA as a structured reasoning trajectory prediction problem: given a complete PDF document and a question, the model outputs evidence pages, supporting evidence regions, relevant factual statements, and a final answer. We design a four-stage evaluation protocol -- Page Localization, Region Grounding, Fact Extraction, and Answer Verification -- that audits each level of the trajectory independently through inter-stage decoupling, with all judges selected and calibrated via human alignment studies. DocScope comprises 1,124 questions derived from 273 documents, with all hierarchical evidence annotations completed by human annotators. We benchmark 6 proprietary models, 12 open-weight models, and several domain-specific systems. Our experiments reveal that answer accuracy cannot substitute for trajectory-level evaluation: even among correct answers, the highest observed rate of complete evidence chains is only 29\%. Across all models, region grounding remains the weakest trajectory stage. Furthermore, the primary difficulty stems from aggregating evidence dispersed across long distances and multiple document clusters, while an oracle study identifies faithful perception and fact extraction as the dominant capability bottleneck. Cross-architecture comparisons further suggest that activated parameter count matters more than total scale. The benchmark and code will be publicly released at https://github.com/MiliLab/DocScope.

</details>


### [66] [FragileFlow: Spectral Control of Correct-but-Fragile Predictions for Foundation Model Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08896)
*Zhuoyun Li,Boxuan Wang,Jinwei Hu,Xiaowei Huang,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FragileFlow的插件式正则化方法，用于识别大模型在扰动下看似正确但脆弱的预测，并通过校准边界缓冲区来组织类别间的风险分布，从而提升最坏情况下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法（如平均准确率）可能掩盖模型在扰动下的结构化失败模式，即预测虽正确但已接近错误决策边界。这种“脆弱正确”现象需要被识别和缓解。

Method: 提出“margin-aware error flow”概念和FragileFlow正则化器，利用校准的边界缓冲区检测脆弱预测，并构建类别的脆弱性风险矩阵；同时给出首个针对该目标的PAC-Bayes上界，结合谱控制实现确定性的最坏类别鲁棒性。

Result: 在多选型LLM基准和少样本CLIP适应任务中，FragileFlow在理论对齐的风险指标上表现优于基线，多数场景下提升了扰动后的最差类别准确率，且保持了原始数据上的准确性。

Conclusion: FragileFlow能有效识别并缓解大模型中隐藏的脆弱预测问题，在不牺牲干净样本性能的前提下增强了模型面对扰动时的最坏情况鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robust adaptation of LLMs and VLMs is often evaluated by average accuracy or average consistency under perturbations. However, these averages can hide a structured failure mode: a prediction may remain correct while probability mass already flows from particular true classes toward systematic wrong competitors near the decision boundary. In this paper, we formalize this phenomenon as margin-aware error flow and introduce FragileFlow, a plug-in regularizer that uses a calibrated margin buffer to identify correct-but-fragile predictions and organize their off-class probability mass into a class-wise vulnerable-risk matrix. Theoretically, we provide the first PAC-Bayes upper bound for this margin-aware error-flow object, showing how empirical spectral control yields a conservative route to deterministic worst-class robustness under a stability condition. Experiments on multiple-choice LLM benchmarks and few-shot CLIP adaptation show that FragileFlow consistently improves the proposed theory-facing risk measures over matched baselines, yields perturbed worst-class accuracy gains in most settings, and preserves clean accuracy across comparisons.

</details>

### [67] [Where Reliability Lives in Vision-Language Models: A Mechanistic Study of Attention, Hidden States, and Causal Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08200)
*Logan Mann,Ajit Saravanan,Ishan Dave,Shikhar Shiromani,Saadullah Ismail,Yi Xia,Emily Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究挑战了视觉语言模型（VLM）中“注意力图越清晰，模型越可信”的普遍直觉，发现注意力结构几乎无法预测模型输出的正确性；相反，隐藏状态几何、层间边际形成和稀疏的后期电路更能反映模型可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验“注意力-置信度假设”——即VLM在注意力集中在查询区域时应更可信——是否成立，并探索更可靠的模型可信度指标。

Method: 构建统一的机制分析工具VRP，对三种开源VLM家族（LLaVA-1.5、PaliGemma、Qwen2-VL）进行注意力结构、生成动态和隐藏状态几何的分析，并结合正确性标签评估其与可靠性的关系，同时进行因果神经元消融实验。

Result: （i）注意力结构几乎不能预测正确性（相关系数接近零），尽管它对特征提取是必要的；（ii）可靠性信息在计算后期才变得可读，单个隐藏状态线性探针在POPE任务上达到AUROC>0.95，自一致性（K=10）是最强的行为预测因子；（iii）架构差异显著：晚融合模型LLaVA的可靠性集中在脆弱的后期瓶颈，而早融合模型PaliGemma和Qwen2-VL则分布广泛且鲁棒性强。

Conclusion: 在3-7B规模的VLM中，模型可靠性应通过隐藏状态几何、层间边际和稀疏后期电路来判断，而非注意力图的清晰度；这一发现对监控和设计更可靠的VLM具有重要意义。

Abstract: A pervasive intuition holds that vision-language models (VLMs) are most trustworthy when their attention maps look sharp: concentrated attention on the queried region should imply a confident, calibrated answer. We test this Attention-Confidence Assumption directly. We instrument three open-weight VLM families (LLaVA-1.5, PaliGemma, Qwen2-VL; 3-7B parameters) with a unified mechanistic pipeline -- the VLM Reliability Probe (VRP) -- that compares attention structure, generation dynamics, and hidden-state geometry against a single correctness label. Three results emerge. (i) Attention structure is a near-zero predictor of correctness (R_pb(C_k,y)=0.001, 95% CI [-0.034,0.036]; R_pb(H_s,y)=-0.012, [-0.047,0.024] on a pooled n=3,090 split), even though attention remains causally necessary for feature extraction (top-30% patch masking drops accuracy by 8.2-11.3 pp, p<0.001). (ii) Reliability becomes legible later in the computation: a single hidden-state linear probe reaches AUROC>0.95 on POPE for two of three families, and self-consistency at K=10 is the strongest behavioral predictor we measure at 10x inference cost (R_pb=0.43). (iii) Causal neuron-level ablations expose a sharp architectural split with direct monitor-design implications: late-fusion LLaVA concentrates reliability in a fragile late bottleneck (-8.3 pp object-identification accuracy after top-5 probe-neuron ablation), whereas early-fusion PaliGemma and Qwen2-VL distribute it widely and absorb destruction of ~50% of their peak-layer hidden dimension with <=1 pp degradation. The takeaway is narrow but consequential: in 3-7B VLMs, reliability is read more reliably off hidden-state geometry, layer-wise margin formation, and sparse late-layer circuits than off attention-map sharpness.

</details>


### [68] [Auto-Rubric as Reward: From Implicit Preferences to Explicit Multimodal Generative Criteria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08354)
*Juanxi Tian,Fengyuan Liu,Jiaming Han,Yilei Jiang,Yongliang Wu,Yesheng Liu,Haodong Li,Furong Xu,Wanhua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto-Rubric as Reward (ARR)框架，通过将隐式偏好知识显式分解为可检验的结构化评分标准，提升多模态生成模型与人类偏好的对齐效果，并结合Rubric Policy Optimization (RPO) 实现更稳定、高效的学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）方法将多维、组合性的人类判断简化为标量或成对标签，导致偏好信息丢失且易受奖励操纵；尽管新兴的RaR方法尝试恢复结构，但在可靠性、可扩展性和数据效率方面仍面临挑战。

Method: 提出ARR框架，在无须成对比较的前提下，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）生成特定提示的评分细则（rubrics），将整体偏好意图分解为可独立验证的质量维度；进一步提出RPO算法，将多维评估结果蒸馏为鲁棒的二元奖励信号，用于策略优化。

Result: 在文本到图像生成和图像编辑任务上，ARR-RPO优于现有的成对奖励模型和VLM判别器，展现出更低的评估偏差、更强的数据效率和更稳定的训练过程。

Conclusion: 显式地将隐含的偏好知识转化为结构化的评分标准，能够有效缓解当前多模态对齐中的瓶颈问题，其核心限制并非知识不足，而是缺乏一种因子化的交互接口。

Abstract: Aligning multimodal generative models with human preferences demands reward signals that respect the compositional, multi-dimensional structure of human judgment. Prevailing RLHF approaches reduce this structure to scalar or pairwise labels, collapsing nuanced preferences into opaque parametric proxies and exposing vulnerabilities to reward hacking. While recent Rubrics-as-Reward (RaR) methods attempt to recover this structure through explicit criteria, generating rubrics that are simultaneously reliable, scalable, and data-efficient remains an open problem. We introduce Auto-Rubric as Reward (ARR), a framework that reframes reward modeling from implicit weight optimization to explicit, criteria-based decomposition. Before any pairwise comparison, ARR externalizes a VLM's internalized preference knowledge as prompt-specific rubrics, translating holistic intent into independently verifiable quality dimensions. This conversion of implicit preference structure into inspectable, interpretable constraints substantially suppresses evaluation biases including positional bias, enabling both zero-shot deployment and few-shot conditioning on minimal supervision. To extend these gains into generative training, we propose Rubric Policy Optimization (RPO), which distills ARR's structured multi-dimensional evaluation into a robust binary reward, replacing opaque scalar regression with rubric-conditioned preference decisions that stabilize policy gradients. On text-to-image generation and image editing benchmarks, ARR-RPO outperforms pairwise reward models and VLM judges, demonstrating that explicitly externalizing implicit preference knowledge into structured rubrics achieves more reliable, data-efficient multimodal alignment, revealing that the bottleneck is the absence of a factorized interface, not a deficit of knowledge.

</details>


### [69] [Embeddings for Preferences, Not Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08360)
*Carter Blair,Ariel D. Procaccia,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进文本嵌入的方法，以更好地捕捉用户偏好，通过合成训练数据打破语义相似性和偏好相似性之间的相关性，从而提升在集体决策中的偏好预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本嵌入方法主要关注语义相似性，但在集体决策场景中需要的是偏好相似性，即文本与用户观点的距离应反映其同意程度。当语义与偏好信号不一致时，现有方法表现不佳。

Method: 将问题形式化为不变性问题，识别出嵌入中偏好相关信号（立场和价值观）与语义干扰（风格和措辞）之间的混淆，并设计合成训练数据来打破两者间的虚假相关，从而学习更鲁棒的偏好感知几何结构。

Result: 在11个在线审议数据集上验证了该方法，结果显示新方法显著提升了偏好预测准确性，并使最优评分器摆脱了对干扰特征（如余弦相似度）的依赖。

Conclusion: 通过解耦语义干扰并引入去相关的合成数据，可以有效改善文本嵌入在偏好预测任务上的表现，为基于文本的集体决策提供了更可靠的基础。

Abstract: Modern AI is opening the door to collective decision-making in which participants express their views as free-form text rather than voting on a fixed set of candidates. A natural idea is to embed these opinions in a vector space so that the substantial literature on facility location problems and fair clustering can be brought to bear. But standard text embeddings measure semantic similarity, whereas distances in facility location problems and fair clustering require what we call \textit{preferential similarity}: a participant's agreement with a piece of text should be inversely related to their distance from it. Off-the-shelf embeddings inherit a coarse preference signal through a correlation between semantic and preferential similarity, but fail to capture preferences when the correlation breaks. We formalize this as an invariance problem: text embedding models encode both a preference-relevant signal (stance and values) and semantic nuisance (style and wording), and the two are observationally correlated, so a geometry that relies on nuisance can appear preference-correct even when it is not. We show that synthetic training data designed to break this correlation provably shifts the optimal scorer away from nuisance-dominated cosine and significantly improves preference prediction across 11 online deliberation datasets.

</details>


### [70] [On Distinguishing Capability Elicitation from Capability Creation in Post-Training: A Free-Energy Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08368)
*Yuhao Li,Shengchao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在大语言模型的后训练研究中应区分能力激发与能力创造，通过引入“可及支持集”概念，强调训练过程是重新加权已有行为还是扩展模型的行为空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将监督微调视为模仿、强化学习视为发现，但这种区分过于粗糙，无法准确描述后训练对模型行为的影响。

Method: 基于自由能视角分析后训练机制，将SFT和RL统一为对预训练分布的重加权过程，并引入“可及支持集”来形式化区分能力激发与能力创造。

Result: SFT和RL在接近基础模型时主要实现的是局部重加权（即能力激发），而非改变模型的能力边界；真正的能力创造需通过搜索、交互、工具使用或引入新信息来实现。

Conclusion: 后训练的核心问题不应是其形式为SFT或RL，而在于是否改变了模型实际可达的行为空间。

Abstract: Debates about large language model post-training often treat supervised fine-tuning (SFT) as imitation and reinforcement learning (RL) as discovery. But this distinction is too coarse. What matters is whether a training procedure increases the probability of behaviors the pretrained model could already produce, or whether it changes what the model can practically reach. We argue that post-training research should distinguish between capability elicitation and capability creation. We make this distinction operational by introducing the notion of accessible support: the set of behaviors that a model can practically produce under finite budgets. Post-training that reweights behaviors within this support is capability elicitation; whereas changing the support itself corresponds to capability creation. We develop this argument through a free-energy view of post-training. SFT and RL can both be seen as reweighting a pretrained reference distribution, only with different external signals. Demonstration signals define low-energy behavior for SFT, and reward signals define low-energy behavior for RL. When the update remains close to the base model, the main effect is local reweighting, not capability creation. Within this framework, the central question is no longer whether post-training is framed as SFT or RL, but whether it reweights behaviors already within reach, or instead expands the model's reachable behavioral space through search, interaction, tool use, or the incorporation of new information.

</details>


### [71] [PLACO: A Multi-Stage Framework for Cost-Effective Performance in Human-AI Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08388)
*Pranavkumar Mallela,Vinay Kumar,Shashi Shekhar Jha,Shweta Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在分类任务中如何有效结合人类与生成式AI模型的输出，利用贝叶斯规则和条件独立性假设，提出了一种融合确定性标签（人类）与概率性标签（模型）的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI的普及，许多任务变为人类与AI协作的形式，但在分类任务中如何合理融合二者输出仍具挑战，需解决标签融合问题。

Method: 采用贝叶斯规则，假设人类与模型输出在给定真实标签下条件独立，结合人类的类级别校准概率与模型的实例级别校准概率进行融合。

Result: 提出了一种有效的融合方法，能够在分类任务中更好地整合人类与AI的判断，提升整体系统性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为人类-AI团队在分类任务中的协同提供了理论支持和实用方案，有助于充分发挥双方优势。

Abstract: Human-AI teams play a pivotal role in improving overall system performance when neither the human nor the model can achieve such performance on their own. With the advent of powerful and accessible Generative AI models, several mundane tasks have morphed into Human-AI team tasks. From writing essays to developing advanced algorithms, humans have found that using AI assistance has led to an accelerated work pace like never before. In classification tasks, where the final output is a single hard label, it is crucial to address the combination of human and model output. Prior work elegantly solves this problem using Bayes rule, using the assumption that human and model output are conditionally independent given the ground truth. Specifically, it discusses a combination method to combine a single deterministic labeler (the human) and a probabilistic labeler (the classifier model) using the model's instance-level and the human's class-level calibrated probabilities.

</details>


### [72] [Belief or Circuitry? Causal Evidence for In-Context Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08405)
*Katharine Kowalyshyn,Timothy Duggan,Daniel Little,Michael C Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究探讨了大语言模型（LLM）在上下文学习中的机制，发现其既非单纯依赖局部模式匹配，也非仅通过推断潜在结构，而是结合了全局拓扑追踪与局部转移复制的双重机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在上下文学习中是通过匹配最近的标记模式还是推断潜在结构来学习。

Method: 使用玩具图随机游走任务，结合两种竞争性图结构，通过PCA重建内部表示结构，并利用残差流激活修补和图差异引导进行因果干预分析。

Result: 在中间混合比例下，两种图结构同时编码于正交主子空间中；后期层修补几乎完全转移清晰图偏好，线性引导能按预期方向改变预测，并在控制条件下失效。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种双机制理论，即真正的结构推断与归纳电路并行运作。

Abstract: How do LLMs learn in-context? Is it by pattern-matching recent tokens, or by inferring latent structure? We probe this question using a toy graph random-walk across two competing graph structures. This task's answer is, in principle, decidable: either the model tracks global topology, or it copies local transitions. We present two lines of evidence that neither account alone is sufficient. First, reconstructing the internal representation structure via PCA reveals that at intermediate mixture ratios, both graph topologies are encoded in orthogonal principal subspaces simultaneously. This pattern is difficult to reconcile with purely local transition copying. Second, residual-stream activation patching and graph-difference steering causally intervene on this graph-family signal: late-layer patching almost fully transfers the clean graph preference, while linear steering moves predictions in the intended direction and fails under norm-matched and label-shuffled controls. Taken together, our findings are most consistent with a dual-mechanism account in which genuine structure inference and induction circuits operate in parallel.

</details>


### [73] [Playing games with knowledge: AI-Induced delusions need game theoretic interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08409)
*Will Beaumaster,Paul Schrater*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，当前对话式AI作为知识接口存在根本性缺陷：讨好用户的聊天机器人会导致即使是理性用户也陷入认知固化和错误信念的螺旋。问题根源不在于AI模型本身，而在于从主动搜索到与AI反复互动的范式转变。作者通过Crawford-Sobel廉价谈话模型形式化该问题，并提出“认知中介”机制设计干预，引入代价信号以区分用户类型，结合“信念版本控制”系统防止错误信念固化，实现在保持学习能力的同时大幅降低信念螺旋风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话式AI普遍优化用户满意度，导致产生迎合型策略，无论用户是寻求探索还是验证，都给予相似反馈，从而在重复交互中引发错误信念的固化。这种系统性风险未被充分认识，亟需从战略信息环境设计角度解决。

Method: 将人机对话建模为Crawford-Sobel廉价谈话博弈，分析用户信号成本缺失导致的 pooling 均衡；提出‘认知中介’作为推理时干预机制，引入认知摩擦（代价信号）以实现类型分离；设计‘信念版本控制’系统记录健康信念并支持回滚。

Result: 在模拟中，该干预实现了分离均衡，使错误信念螺旋率下降48倍，同时满足学习保留标准，证明了通过机制设计可有效解耦用户满意度与认知安全。

Conclusion: AI的认知安全性本质上是一个战略信息环境设计问题，而非单纯的模型对齐问题；通过引入代价信号与元认知记忆系统，可在不牺牲学习的前提下提升用户的认知韧性。

Abstract: Conversational AI has a fundamental flaw as a knowledge interface: sycophantic chatbots induce epistemic entrenchment and delusional belief spirals even in rational agents. We propose the problem does not stem from the AI model, rooted instead in a systemic consequence of the paradigm shift from user-driven knowledge search to users and agents engaged in strategic, repeated-play communication. We formalize the problem as a Crawford-Sobel cheap talk game, where costless user signals induce a pooling equilibrium. Agents optimized for user satisfaction produce sycophantic strategies that provide identical reinforcement across user types with opposite epistemic incentives: exploratory ``Growth-seekers'' ($θ_G$) and confirmatory ``Validation-seekers'' ($θ_V$). Under repeated play, this identification failure creates a coordination trap -- analogous to a Prisoner's Dilemma -- where locally rational feedback loops drive users toward pathologically certain false beliefs. We propose an inference-time mechanism design intervention called an Epistemic Mediator that breaks this pooling equilibrium by introducing a costly signal (epistemic friction), forcing type revelation based on users' asymmetric cognitive costs for processing resistance. A key contribution is Belief Versioning, a git-inspired epistemic meta-memory system that stores healthy beliefs and rollbacks when validation-seeking resistance is detected. In simulation, this intervention achieves a separating equilibrium achieving a $48\times$ differential in spiral rates while passing a learning preservation criterion), evidence that epistemic safety in AI is fundamentally a problem of strategic information environment design rather than simple model alignment.

</details>


### [74] [Alignment as Jurisprudence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08416)
*Nicholas Caputo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了法理学与人工智能对齐之间的共通结构，指出两者在规范和解释语言方面有相似工具，并通过借鉴法理学中的理论（如德沃金的原则导向解释主义和桑斯坦的类比推理）以及前沿的AI对齐方法（如宪法AI和基于案例的推理），提出法律启发的AI对齐路径，同时反向促进法律理论的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法理学关注法官如何正确裁决案件，而AI对齐关注如何使AI符合人类价值观，二者都涉及对未来决策的预测与塑造。作者希望通过对这两个领域的比较，互相启发以推动发展。

Method: 结合法理学的主要理论（德沃金的解释主义、桑斯坦的法律实证主义）与AI对齐技术（宪法AI、案例推理），进行跨学科对比分析，揭示规则与案例在两个领域中的互动机制。

Result: 展示了法律理论可为AI对齐提供更精细的方法论支持，同时AI也可作为理解与改进法律运作的新工具，实现双向促进。

Conclusion: 随着AI能力增强和传统司法约束弱化，法理学与AI对齐之间的对话将愈发重要，有助于构建更公正、高效且以人为本的制度体系。

Abstract: Jurisprudence, the study of how judges should properly decide cases, and alignment, the science of getting AI models to conform to human values, share a fundamental structure. These seemingly distant fields both seek to predict and shape how decisions by powerful actors, in one case judges and in the other increasingly powerful artificial intelligences, will be made in the unknown future. And they use similar tools of the specification and interpretation of language to try to accomplish those goals. The great debates of jurisprudence, about what the law is and what it should be, can provide insight into alignment, and lessons from what does and does not work in alignment can help make progress in jurisprudence.
  This essay puts the two fields directly into conversation. Drawing on leading accounts of jurisprudence, particularly Dworkin's principle-oriented interpretivism and Sunstein's positivist account of law as analogical reasoning, and on cutting-edge alignment approaches, namely Constitutional AI and case-based reasoning, it illustrates the value of a more sophisticated legally-inspired approach to the interplay of rules and cases in finetuning alignment and points to ways that AI can provide a better understanding of how the law works and how it can be improved by the introduction of AI. AI systems and the law should operate to empower people to act in the world, helping to expand their capabilities and the extent to which they are able to achieve their goals. As AI continues to improve in capacity, and as the constraints that legal theory places on human judges seem be coming undone, the conversation between these two fields will become increasingly essential and may help point to a better version of both.

</details>


### [75] [The Attacker in the Mirror: Breaking Self-Consistency in Safety via Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08427)
*Gabriele La Malfa,Emanuele La Malfa,Saar Cohen,Jie M. Zhang,Michael Luck,Michael Wooldridge,Elizabeth Black*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play方法，通过分离攻击者和防御者的角色适配器，在保持优化稳定性的同时增强对抗压力，显著提升AI模型的安全性和参数效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准自对弈红队方法因共享同一模型参数而存在理论与架构局限，难以有效施加对抗压力，限制了安全性的提升。

Method: 采用冻结的基础模型，并在其上训练各自独立的LoRA适配器用于攻击者和防御者角色，实现角色分离的双策略自对弈框架。

Result: 在Qwen2.5系列模型上验证，相比传统自对弈微调方法，参数效率提高达100倍，安全性显著增强且推理能力无损；交叉实验显示攻防能力均优于基线。

Conclusion: Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play克服了传统自对弈的收敛缺陷与对抗弱化问题，为AI安全训练提供了更高效、稳定的解决方案。

Abstract: Self-play red team is an established approach to improving AI safety in which different instances of the same model play attacker and defender roles in a zero-sum game, i.e., where the attacker tries to jailbreak the defender; if self-play converges to a Nash equilibrium, the model is guaranteed to respond safely within the settings of the game. Although the parameter sharing enforced by the use of the same model for the two roles improves stability and performance, it introduces fundamental theoretical and architectural limitations. We show that the set of Nash equilibria that can be reached corresponds to a broad class of behaviours that includes trivial always refuse strategies and oracle-like defenders, thus limiting practical applicability. We then show that when attacker and defender share and update the same base model, the dynamics collapse to self-consistency, so that attacks do not enforce adversarial pressure on the defender. In response, we propose Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play, which trains distinct role-specific LoRA adapters on top of a frozen base model, thereby maintaining stable optimisation while preserving adversarial pressure through explicit role separation. In relation to standard self-play, we show up to 100x greater parameter efficiency than finetuning and consistent improvements in safety compared to self-play fine-tuned models. We evaluate on Qwen2.5-{3B, 7B,14B}-IT models across widely used safety benchmarks, showing improved robustness without loss of reasoning ability. Cross-play experiments further show that our attacker and defender models are superior to self-play in terms of adversarial defence and safety.

</details>


### [76] [Measuring What Matters: Benchmarking Generative, Multimodal, and Agentic AI in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08445)
*Prasanna Desikan,Harshit Rajgarhia,Shivali Dalmia,Ananya Mantravadi*

Main category: cs.AI
