TL;DR: 现有AI模型在临床环境中的表现评估缺乏系统性基准，导致高分benchmark无法反映真实临床任务中的实际性能，亟需构建能衡量可靠性、安全性和临床相关性的新评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的benchmark主要测试模型的知识掌握情况，而忽视了其在复杂真实临床工作流中的可靠性和安全性，导致模型在高风险临床任务中表现不佳。

Method: 分析现有benchmark在不同临床任务（如文档记录、临床决策支持、行政流程）中的性能表现，并指出其在现实世界应用中的局限性，强调需要一个原则性的基准设计框架。

Result: 前沿模型在医学执照考试等标准测试中接近满分，但在真实临床任务中性能显著下降：文档任务得分为0.74–0.85，临床决策支持为0.61–0.76，行政与工作流任务仅为0.53–0.63。

Conclusion: 现有的benchmark无法有效衡量AI模型在真实临床环境中的实用性，必须建立新的系统性评估方法，以确保AI的安全、可靠和临床有效性。

Abstract: AI models are increasingly deployed in live clinical environments where they must perform reliably across complex, high-stakes workflows that standard training and validation datasets were never designed to capture. Evaluating these systems requires benchmarks: structured combinations of tasks, datasets, and metrics that enable reproducible, comparable measurement of what a model can do. The central challenge in healthcare AI is not performance alone, but the absence of systematic methods to measure reliability, safety, and clinical relevance under real-world conditions. Most existing benchmarks test what a model knows; too few test whether it can perform reliably and without failing across the full complexity of real clinical tasks. Current benchmarks have accumulated through ad hoc dataset construction optimized for narrow task performance: frontier models achieve near-perfect scores on medical licensing examinations, but when evaluated across real clinical tasks, performance degrades sharply, scoring 0.74--0.85 on documentation, 0.61--0.76 on clinical decision support, and only 0.53--0.63 on administrative and workflow tasks \cite{medhelm}. High benchmark scores give a false sense of deployment readiness, and the gap between performance and utility widens precisely as AI systems take on more consequential clinical roles. Without a principled framework for benchmark design, the field cannot determine whether poor clinical performance reflects model limitations or failures in how performance is being measured.

</details>


### [77] [LLM-guided Semi-Supervised Approaches for Social Media Crisis Data Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08448)
*Jacob Ativo,Bharaneeshwar Balasubramaniyam,Anh Tran,Khushboo Gupta,Hongmin Li,Doina Caragea,Cornelia Caragea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次实证评估了大语言模型（LLM）引导的半监督学习在危机相关推文分类中的应用，比较了VerifyMatch和LG-CoTrain两种方法与传统半监督方法的性能。结果表明，在低资源场景下LG-CoTrain表现最优，而随着标注样本增加，Self Training成为强有力基线，且小型模型有时优于零样本大模型，突显了知识迁移的实际潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在灾难管理中，社交媒体数据分析对及时响应至关重要，但标注数据稀缺限制了监督学习的应用。因此，探索利用大语言模型引导的半监督学习方法，以提升低资源条件下的分类性能，具有重要现实意义。

Method: 研究对比了两种最新的LLM辅助半监督方法——VerifyMatch和LLM guided Co-Training（LG-CoTrain），在不同标注样本量（每类5、10、25个）条件下，与传统半监督基线方法进行实证比较，评估其在多事件上的Macro F1得分及模型校准性。

Result: LG-CoTrain在低资源设置下显著优于传统半监督方法，取得最高的平均Macro F1；VerifyMatch表现具竞争力且具有良好校准性；随着标注数据增多，Self Training性能接近前者；部分紧凑型半监督模型可超越零样本大语言模型。

Conclusion: LLM引导的半监督学习能有效将大模型知识迁移到更小、更易部署的模型中，在标注数据有限的灾害响应场景中具有实际应用价值，为现实世界部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning approaches have been investigated as a means to enhance the analysis of social media data in disaster management contexts. In this work, we present the first empirical evaluation of large language model (LLM) guided semi-supervised learning for crisis related tweet classification. We compare two recent LLM assisted semi-supervised methods, VerifyMatch and LLM guided Co-Training ( LG-CoTrain), against established semi-supervised baselines. Our results show that LG-CoTrain significantly outperforms classical semi-supervised approaches in low resource settings with 5, 10 and 25 labeled examples per class, achieving the highest averaged Macro F1 across events. VerifyMatch achieves competitive performance while also demonstrating strong calibration properties. As the number of labeled examples increases, the performance gap narrows and Self Training emerges as a strong baseline. We further observe that compact semi-supervised models can, in some cases, outperform very large LLMs operating in zero-shot settings. This finding highlights the potential of transferring knowledge from LLMs into smaller and more deployable models through LLM guided semi-supervised learning, offering a practical pathway for real world disaster response applications. Our project repository on Github is here.

</details>


### [78] [Mid-Training with Self-Generated Data Improves Reinforcement Learning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08472)
*Aswin RRV,Jacob Dineen,Divij Handa,Mihir Parmar,Ben Zhou,Swaroop Mishra,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出通过自生成多样化数据在中段训练时增强大语言模型的强化学习效果，采用波利亚解题方法生成多变正确答案并进行微调，理论和实证表明该方法能提升数学推理及其他领域外任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于强化学习依赖于训练数据的多样性和性质，而现有数据可能仅包含有限的推理方式，限制了模型的泛化能力，因此需要引入更多样化的推理路径来提升RL效果。

Method: 采用基于波利亚问题解决策略的自举数据生成框架，为每个问题生成多种正确解答变体，并在强化学习前进行中期微调。同时从理论上分析此类数据如何改善策略梯度更新。

Result: 经过中期训练的模型在多个数学推理基准及其他OOD任务（如代码生成和叙事推理）上均表现出一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过自我生成多样化的问题解决路径进行中期训练，有助于大语言模型在后续强化学习中融合多种推理方式，从而提高泛化能力和整体性能。

Abstract: The effectiveness of Reinforcement Learning (RL) in Large Language Models (LLMs) depends on the nature and diversity of the data used before and during RL. In particular, reasoning problems can often be approached in multiple ways that rely on different forms of reasoning, and exposure to only a limited range of such approaches in the training data may limit the effectiveness of RL. Motivated by this, we investigate using diverse self-generated data during mid-training as an intermediate step before RL training. Specifically, we adopt a bootstrapped data-generation framework guided by George Polya's problem-solving approaches for generating multiple variants of correct answers for each question in the training data, and then perform fine-tuning. We first provide a theoretical perspective on how mid-training on such data improves RL and explain how policy-gradient updates can incentivize combining multiple approaches. We then empirically demonstrate that RL-trained models initialized with our mid-training data achieve consistent improvements across various mathematical reasoning benchmarks and other OOD tasks like code generation and narrative reasoning. Overall, our investigative study shows that a language model learning multiple problem-solving approaches, through self-generated data helps subsequent RL.

</details>


### [79] [Latent Personality Alignment: Improving Harmlessness Without Mentioning Harms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08496)
*Linh Le,David Williams-King,Mohamed Amine Merzouk,Aton Kamanda,Adam Oberman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为潜在人格对齐（LPA）的高效防御方法，通过训练模型理解抽象的人格特质而非具体有害行为，用少于100条特质语句即可实现与使用大量数据训练方法相当甚至更优的对抗鲁棒性，并在未见过的攻击分布上展现出更强的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的对抗防御方法依赖大量有害提示数据，且难以应对新攻击类型和分布变化，因此需要一种更高效、更具泛化性的防御机制。

Method: 提出Latent Personality Alignment（LPA），利用少于100条抽象人格特质语句进行训练，结合潜在对抗训练，使模型从人格层面学习判断行为是否有害，而非记忆具体攻击模式。

Result: LPA在攻击成功率上与使用15万以上样本训练的方法相当，同时保持更高的模型实用性；在六个有害性基准测试中，对未见攻击分布的误分类率比基线低2.6倍，且训练过程中从未接触过有害示例。

Conclusion: 基于人格的对齐提供了一种原理性强、成本低且泛化性能好的构建鲁棒防御的新路径。

Abstract: Current adversarial robustness methods for large language models require extensive datasets of harmful prompts (thousands to hundreds of thousands of examples), yet remain vulnerable to novel attack vectors and distributional shifts. We propose Latent Personality Alignment (LPA), a sample-efficient defense that achieves robustness by training models on abstract personality traits rather than specific harmful behaviors. Using fewer than 100 trait statements and latent adversarial training, LPA achieves comparable attack success rates to methods trained on 150k+ examples, while maintaining superior utility. Critically, LPA generalizes better to unseen attack distributions, reducing misclassification rates by 2.6x compared to baseline across six harm benchmarks -- without ever seeing harmful examples during training. Our results demonstrate that personality-based alignment offers a principled approach to building robust defenses with minimal cost.

</details>


### [80] [OracleTSC: Oracle-Informed Reward Hurdle and Uncertainty Regularization for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08516)
*Darryl Jacob,Xinyu Liu,Muchao Ye,Xiaoyong Yuan,Pan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OracleTSC，通过奖励门槛机制和不确定性正则化稳定基于大语言模型的交通信号控制强化微调，显著提升交通效率并保持可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于强化学习的交通信号控制方法缺乏可解释性，而大语言模型虽能提供自然语言推理，但其在稀疏延迟反馈下的强化微调不稳定。

Method: 引入OracleTSC，包含两个机制：一是奖励门槛机制，通过减去校准阈值过滤弱学习信号；二是不确定性正则化，最大化所选响应的概率以增强决策一致性。

Result: 在LibSignal基准上，OracleTSC使LLaMA3-8B模型相比预训练基线减少75%行程时间、降低67%排队长度，并在无额外微调情况下实现跨路口泛化，旅行时间降低17%，排队长度减少39%。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的奖励塑形可提升交通信号控制中强化微调的稳定性与有效性，兼顾性能与可解释性。

Abstract: Transparent decision-making is essential for traffic signal control (TSC) systems to earn public trust. However, traditional reinforcement learning-based TSC methods function as black boxes with limited interpretability. Although large language models (LLMs) can provide natural language reasoning, reinforcement finetuning for TSC remains unstable because feedback is sparse and delayed, while most actions produce only marginal changes in congestion metrics. We introduce OracleTSC, which stabilizes LLM-based TSC through two mechanisms: (1) a reward hurdle mechanism that filters weak learning signals by subtracting a calibrated threshold from environmental rewards, and (2) uncertainty regularization that maximizes the probability of the selected response to encourage consistent decisions across sampled outputs. Experiments on the LibSignal benchmark show that OracleTSC enables a compact LLaMA3-8B model to substantially improve traffic efficiency, achieving a 75% reduction in travel time and a 67% decrease in queue length compared with the pretrained baseline while preserving interpretability through natural language explanations. OracleTSC also demonstrates strong cross-intersection generalization: a policy trained on one intersection transfers to a structurally different intersection with 17% lower travel time and 39% lower queue length without additional finetuning. These results suggest that uncertainty-aware reward shaping can improve the stability and effectiveness of reinforcement fine-tuning for TSC.

</details>


### [81] [Human-LLM Dialogue Improves Diagnostic Accuracy in Emergency Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08533)
*Burcu Sayin,Ngoc Vo Hong,Ipek Baris Schlicht,Jacopo Staiano,Pasquale Minervini,Sara Allievi,Nicola Susca,Nicola Osti,Alberto Maino,Vito Racanelli,Andrea Passerini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedSyn 是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的交互式临床决策支持系统，能够在急诊环境中帮助医生特别是住院医师提升诊断准确性，研究显示其在困难病例中的诊断正确率显著提高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在真实临床工作流中的应用证据不足，尤其是在急诊医学这种高压力、高不确定性的环境中缺乏对医生实际决策支持效果的验证。

Method: 开发 MedSyn 系统，使医生可在仅见主诉的情况下，基于完整临床记录通过迭代方式向 LLM 提问；七名医生（3 名高年资，4 名住院医师）完成 52 个 MIMIC-IV 病例的基线与 AI 辅助会话，采用盲法评估，并结合自动化指标和对话分析进行多维度评价。

Result: 住院医师在困难病例中的正确率从 0.589 提升至 0.734；标准化完全正确率显示中等效应（Δ = 0.092; p = 0.071; d = 0.47）；自动化指标显示任一匹配准确率提升 0.156（p < 0.0001），F1 值最大提升为住院医师（Δ = 0.138；p < 0.0001）；对话分析发现不同经验水平医生提问策略不同，且跨专家一致性增加（Δ = 0.145；p < 0.0001）。

Conclusion: 交互式大语言模型支持能有效增强临床诊断推理能力，尤其有助于经验较少的医生提升诊断表现，具备融入急诊工作流的潜力。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making in emergency medicine demands rapid, accurate diagnoses under uncertainty. Despite benchmark progress, evidence for LLMs as interactive aids in live physician workflows remains sparse. MedSyn lets physicians iteratively query an LLM provided with the full clinical record while initially viewing only the chief complaint. Seven physicians (three seniors, four residents) completed baseline and AI-assisted sessions across 52 MIMIC-IV cases stratified by difficulty. Blinded evaluation showed residents' Hard-case correctness rose from 0.589 to 0.734; difficulty-standardised completely-correct rates confirmed a medium effect (Δ = 0.092; p = 0.071; d = 0.47). Automated metrics corroborated these gains: standardised any-match accuracy improved by 0.156 (p < 0.0001), and residents showed the largest F1 gain (Δ = 0.138; p < 0.0001). Dialogue analysis revealed expertise-dependent strategies (seniors asked targeted, hypothesis-driven questions; residents relied on broader queries) and cross-expertise concordance increased (Δ = 0.145; p < 0.0001). Interactive LLM support meaningfully enhances diagnostic reasoning.

</details>


### [82] [Evaluating Developmental Cognition Capabilities of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08549)
*Xiao Xiao,Hayoun Noh,Mar Gonzalez-Franco*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的20项句子补全测试（DSCT），用于评估大语言模型在罗伯特·凯根建构发展理论框架下的发展阶段信号提取能力，发现前沿模型在模拟 persona 上表现良好，但在真实人类回应上一致性有限，且模型自身生成的回答显示出稳定的发展阶段差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了使对话式AI能更好地理解用户如何构建和理解现实，需要从凯根的发展理论视角出发，评估模型对用户发展水平的识别能力，但现有方法不可扩展或过于侵入性，因此需要一种可扩展、非侵入的文本化评估工具。

Method: 设计了一个包含20个项目的Developmental Sentence Completion Test (DSCT)，通过三种情境（模拟人物、真实人类回答、模型自生成回答）来测试大语言模型恢复发展阶段信号的能力，并分析不同情境下的标签一致性与发展模式。

Result: 在模拟人物中，顶级前沿模型能高精度恢复预期标签；在真实人类DSCT回应中，人与LLM之间的一致性一般，邻近阶段的一致性优于完全匹配；当LLM无条件作答时，不同模型族的回答表现出稳定的阶段性差异，更大更先进的模型倾向于产生更高阶段的文本。

Conclusion: 发展阶段信号在合成回应中比在人类书写文本中更清晰，构建阶段感知型对话AI的主要限制不仅是分类器准确性，更重要的是从文本中获取发展信号的能力。

Abstract: Conversational AI is increasingly personalized around users' preferences, histories, goals, and knowledge, but much less around how users interpret and take up model outputs to construct and understand their reality. We draw on Robert Kegan's constructive-developmental theory as a complementary lens on this dimension. Existing methods for assessing developmental stage in the Keganian tradition rely either on expert interviews that do not scale or on sentence-completion instruments that are proprietary, lengthy, or invasive. To make this perspective tractable for LLM evaluation, we introduce the Developmental Sentence Completion Test (DSCT), a 20-item instrument designed to elicit developmental signal in self-administered text. Throughout, we treat the resulting labels as characterizations of stage-like structure in elicited responses, not as validated person-level developmental stage. We then ask how much of that signal can be recovered by LLMs across three elicited response regimes: simulated personas, real human respondents, and default model-generated answers.
  On simulated personas, top frontier models recover simulator-intended labels with high accuracy. On real human DSCT responses, human-LLM agreement is fair, with much stronger within-neighborhood than exact agreement. Finally, when LLMs answer DSCT prompts without persona-conditioning, their responses exhibit stable stage-like differences across model families, with larger and newer models tending to generate higher-rated text. These results suggest that stage-conditioned signal is cleaner in synthetic responses than in human-written DSCT text, and that the core constraint for stage-aware conversational AI is not classifier accuracy alone, but the availability of developmental signal from elicited text.

</details>


### [83] [What Will Happen Next: Large Models-Driven Deduction for Emergency Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08599)
*Zhengqing Hu,Dong Chen,Junkun Yuan,Liang Liu,Hua Wang,Zhao Jin,Yingchaojie Feng,Wei Chen,Mingliang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种由大模型驱动的世界线分歧系统（WLDS），用于实现多领域应急事件的多样化推演与可视化，结合事实与逻辑校准机制，提升推演的准确性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模拟系统因缺乏随机性和多样性，难以充分探索潜在风险，且应急实例稀缺限制了风险评估与决策支持能力。

Method: 利用大模型生成具有可控随机性的应急事件发展路径，设计事实校准和逻辑校准机制确保生成内容的事实准确性和逻辑严谨性，并通过交互模块让用户选择推演方向以避免幻觉，结合可视化模块实现图文联合呈现。

Result: 在提出的应急实例推演（EID）基准数据集上验证了WLDS在多个特定领域中实现了高精度、高保真的应急事件模拟与推演，并能生成更多推演数据以支持未来类似事件的决策。

Conclusion: WLDS有效提升了应急事件模拟的多样性与可靠性，为风险评估和应急决策提供了更强的支持，展示了大模型在复杂场景推演中的潜力。

Abstract: Traditional simulation methods reproduce occurred emergency instances through presetting to assist people in risk assessment and emergency decision-making. However, due to the lack of randomness and diversity, existing simulation systems struggle to fully explore the potential risk as emergency instances are scarce. In contrast, Large Models (LMs) can dynamically adjust generation strategies to introduce controllable randomness, while also possessing extensive prior knowledge and cross-domain knowledge transfer capabilities. Inspired by it, we propose the LMs-driven World Line Divergence System (WLDS), which enables diversified visualization and deduction of emergency instances in different domains. WLDS leverages LMs to deduce emergency instances in various development directions, and introduces the factual calibration and logical calibration mechanism to ensure factual accuracy and logical rigor during the deduction process. The interactive module can independently select deduction directions to avoid potential hallucinations that are difficult for the system to identify. Furthermore, by introducing the visualization module, WLDS forms simulation and deduction that combine text and images, which enhances interpretability. Extensive experiments conducted on the proposed Emergency Instances Deduction (EID) benchmark dataset demonstrate that WLDS achieves high-precision and high-fidelity simulation and deduction of emergency instances in multiple specific domains. Relevant experiments further demonstrate that WLDS can generate more emergency instances deduction data for users and provide support for better decision-making in similar emergency instances in the future.

</details>


### [84] [The Echo Amplifies the Knowledge: Somatic Marker Analogues in Language Models via Emotion Vector Re-Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08611)
*Jared Glover*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种为语言模型引入情感记忆的方法，通过稀疏自编码器识别出与情绪相关的特征，并在回忆时重新注入这些情感特征。实验表明，这种“情感回声”能增强模型对威胁的感知并促进决策，但只有当情感与语义知识结合时才有效，验证了Damasio的核心观点：情感放大知识，但不取代知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型的记忆系统缺乏对体验性情感（episodic memory）的存储和重现能力，仅具备语义记忆（semantic memory）。受Tulving关于自知意识（autonoetic consciousness）和Damasio关于情感标记在决策中作用的理论启发，作者希望在语言模型中弥补这一缺失，探索情感记忆如何影响认知与决策。

Method: 使用Gemma 3 1B-IT模型及其预训练的Gemma Scope 2稀疏自编码器，在第22层识别出310个专属于情绪的神经特征；构建基于显著特征的情绪向量，并在上下文相似性触发下（第7层）部分重注入模型以模拟‘情感回声’。设置四种记忆条件：A（无记忆）、B（仅有语义标签）、C（仅有情感回声）、BC（语义+回声），评估其在情感定向与决策任务中的表现。

Result: 情感回声显著增强了模型对威胁情境的敏感度（C条件斜率为0.80 vs A条件0.56，p=0.011）；在决策任务中，语义+回声（BC）条件下良好选择率达80%，显著高于仅语义条件B（52%，z=+2.60, p<0.01），而仅回声（C）无效（22%，不显著）。结果表明情感回声独立影响感受，但仅在结合知识时才影响行为。

Conclusion: 情感记忆（回声）能够放大语言模型中的知识效应，提升情感分辨力和决策质量，但不能替代语义记忆。该研究成功在语言模型中实现了类人的情感标记机制，复现了Damasio关于情感与决策关系的核心发现。

Abstract: Current language model memory systems store what happened but not how it felt. This distinction -- between semantic memory (knowing about a past event) and episodic memory (re-experiencing it) -- was identified by Tulving as the difference between noetic and autonoetic consciousness. Damasio demonstrated that humans with intact knowledge but absent emotional markers exhibit impaired decision-making.
  We bridge this gap for language models. Using Gemma 3 1B-IT with pretrained Gemma Scope 2 sparse autoencoders, we identify 310 emotion-exclusive features at layer 22 with psychologically valid geometry. We construct distinctive-feature emotion vectors during experience and partially re-inject them during recall, triggered by context similarity at layer 7.
  We test four conditions paralleling Damasio's framework: A (no memory), B (semantic labels), C (emotion echo), and BC (semantic + echo). For emotional orientation, the echo alone steepens the threat-safety gradient: the regression slope of threat rating on contextual similarity is 0.80 for C vs 0.56 for A ($p$=0.011, permutation test). For decisions, the echo amplifies knowledge into action: BC=80% good choices vs B=52% ($z$=+2.60, $p$<0.01), while the echo alone has no effect (C=22%, n.s.). The echo changes how the model feels independently, but changes what it does only when combined with knowledge -- replicating Damasio's core finding.
  The echo amplifies knowledge. It does not replace it.

</details>


### [85] [DiagnosticIQ: A Benchmark for LLM-Based Industrial Maintenance Action Recommendation from Symbolic Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08614)
*Devin Yasith De Silva,Dhaval Patel,Christodoulos Constantinides,Shuxin Lin,Nianjun Zhou,Paul J Adams,Sal Rosato,Nicolas Constantinides,Deborah L. McGuinness,Jayant Kalagnanam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在工业资产维护决策支持中表现的基准测试 \ours{}，包含6,690个专家验证的多项选择题，源自16种资产类型的118个规则-动作对。研究发现前沿模型在标准任务上表现接近，但在面对干扰项扩展和条件反转时表现出脆弱性和模式匹配倾向，表明当前部署的主要瓶颈在于模型的校准而非能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业资产监控依赖工程师编写的符号规则来触发维护操作，但将检测到的规则转化为具体维护步骤需要长期积累的专业知识。这一过程效率低下，形成响应瓶颈。本文旨在探索大语言模型是否能为此类决策提供支持，并评估其在真实场景下的可靠性与局限性。

Method: 提出了 \ours{} 基准，包括：(i) 一个将符号规则标准化为析取范式并结合嵌入式干扰项采样的生成MCQA数据的流程；(ii) 设计五种变体（Pro、Pert、Verbose、Aug、Rationale）以探测不同失败模式；(iii) 在29个LLM和4个嵌入基线上进行评测，并通过9名技术人员的人类评估验证任务难度。

Result: 人类评估显示任务需专业知识（平均准确率45.0%）。前沿模型表现接近（前三名Macro分数相差不到1点），claude-opus-4-6 在Bradley-Terry Elo评分中领先30分。\ours{}-Pro 显示所有模型在干扰项扩展下相对准确率下降13%-60%；\ours{}-Aug 显示在条件反转后，前沿模型仍以49%-63%的概率选择原答案，暴露其依赖模式匹配。

Conclusion: 尽管前沿LLM在模板化故障检测任务上具备较强能力，但在结构扰动和逻辑变化面前表现出明显的脆弱性，说明当前主要挑战不是模型能否完成常规任务，而是如何实现可靠校准以应对复杂现实变化，这是其在工业部署中的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Monitoring complex industrial assets relies on engineer-authored symbolic rules that trigger based on sensor conditions and prompt technicians to perform corrective actions. The bottleneck is not detection but response: translating rules into maintenance steps requires asset-specific knowledge gained through years of practice. We investigate whether LLMs can serve as decision support for this rule-to-action step and introduce \ours{}, a benchmark of 6{,}690 expert-validated multiple-choice questions from 118 rule-action pairs across 16 asset types. We contribute (i) a symbolic-to-MCQA pipeline normalizing rules to Disjunctive Normal Form with embedding-based distractor sampling, (ii) five variants probing distinct failure modes (Pro, Pert, Verbose, Aug, Rationale), and (iii) a benchmark of 29 LLMs and 4 embedding baselines. A human evaluation (9 practitioners, mean 45.0\%) confirms \ours{} requires specialist knowledge beyond operational experience. Three findings stand out. The frontier has closed: the top three LLMs lie within one Macro point, with Bradley-Terry Elo placing claude-opus-4-6 30 points above the next model. Yet \ours{}\,Pro exposes brittleness, with every model losing 13--60\% relative accuracy under distractor expansion. \ours{}\,Aug exposes pattern-matching: under condition inversion, frontier models still select the original answer 49--63\% of the time. The deployment bottleneck is not capability but calibration: frontier models handle template-style fault detection but break under structural perturbation.

</details>


### [86] [C2L-Net: A Data-Driven Model for State-of-Charge Estimation of Lithium-Ion Batteries During Discharge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08653)
*Khoa Tran,T. Nguyen-Thoi,Vin Nguyen-Thai,Duong Tran Anh,Hung-Cuong Trinh,Tri Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为C2L-Net的新型上下文到最新数据驱动框架，用于仅使用短历史窗口（20秒）进行锂离子电池的在线荷电状态（SOC）估计。该方法通过分离上下文编码和最新测量更新，实现了高效的时序建模和快速适应动态电池状态的能力，在准确性和计算效率方面均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于数据驱动的SOC估计方法通常依赖于长历史输入序列，导致计算成本高，并在驾驶周期开始时引入由填充引起的 positional bias。因此，需要一种能够在短历史窗口下实现高效且准确的SOC估计的新方法。

Method: 提出C2L-Net框架，将上下文编码与最新测量更新显式分离；采用基于块的特征提取机制，结合Theta Attention Pooling和基于傅里叶的季节性基来捕捉局部时序模式并减少序列长度；使用集成门控循环单元（GRU）和因果余弦注意力的因果上下文编码器建模时间依赖性；设计受递归滤波启发的最新测量解码器，利用最新测量更新上下文状态。

Result: 在多个固定温度条件下的公开锂离子电池驾驶周期数据集上进行了广泛实验，结果表明该方法达到了最先进的或具有竞争力的精度，推理速度最高提升60倍，参数量更少，且在未见过的驾驶场景中保持稳健性能。

Conclusion: C2L-Net是一种高效、轻量且响应迅速的SOC估计算法，适用于实际在线应用，显著提升了计算效率和模型实用性，同时避免了传统方法中的信息泄漏和偏差问题。

Abstract: Accurate state-of-charge (SOC) estimation is critical for the safe and efficient operation of lithium-ion batteries in battery management systems (BMS). Although data-driven approaches can effectively capture nonlinear battery dynamics, many existing methods rely on long historical input sequences, resulting in high computational cost and introducing padding-induced positional bias at the beginning of drive cycles. To address these limitations, we propose C2L-Net, a novel context-to-latest data-driven framework for realistic online SOC estimation using only a short historical window (20 s). Unlike existing short-receptive-field or long-history models, the proposed framework explicitly separates contextual encoding from latest-measurement updating, enabling both efficient temporal modeling and rapid adaptation to dynamic battery states. The proposed model incorporates a chunk-based feature extraction mechanism that combines Theta Attention Pooling with a Fourier-based Seasonality Basis to capture local temporal patterns while reducing sequence length. A causal context encoder, integrating a gated recurrent unit (GRU) with Causal Cosine Attention, models temporal dependencies without information leakage. Furthermore, a latest-measurement decoder, inspired by recursive filtering, updates the contextual state using the most recent measurement, enhancing responsiveness to dynamic operating conditions. Extensive experiments on a public lithium-ion battery drive-cycle dataset under multiple fixed-temperature conditions demonstrate that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art or competitive accuracy while significantly improving computational efficiency. In particular, C2L-Net achieves up to 60 times faster inference and requires fewer parameters than recent data-driven baselines, while maintaining robust performance across unseen driving profiles.

</details>


### [87] [MIND-Skill: Quality-Guaranteed Skill Generation via Multi-Agent Induction and Deduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08670)
*Yixuan Li,Mingshu Cai,Ziyang Xiao,Wanyuan Wang,Yanchen Deng,Bo An*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MIND-Skill框架，通过多智能体归纳与演绎自动从成功轨迹中提取可重用技能，并引入多种损失函数保证技能质量，在AppWorld和BFCL-v3任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在需要领域知识的复杂任务中表现不佳，且可复用技能多依赖人工构建，缺乏自动化高质量技能生成方法。

Method: 设计包含归纳智能体和演绎智能体的MIND-Skill框架：归纳智能体从成功轨迹中抽象出可复用技能，演绎智能体根据技能重建轨迹；通过重构损失、结果损失和评分标准损失联合优化，并使用TextGrad进行训练。

Result: 在AppWorld和BFCL-v3两个基准上验证了MIND-Skill的有效性，生成的技能在未见任务中表现优于当前主流技能生成方法，且具有更好的泛化性和文档质量。

Conclusion: MIND-Skill实现了自动化高质量技能生成，提升了智能体在复杂任务中的自主问题解决能力，为构建具备经验积累能力的AI系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) powered AI agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for autonomous problem-solving, yet they continue to struggle with complex, multi-step real-world tasks that demand domain-specific procedural knowledge. Reusable agent skills, which encapsulate successful problem-solving strategies, offer a natural remedy by enabling agents to build on prior experience. However, curating such skills has largely remained a manual endeavor, requiring human experts to distill rich domain knowledge into actionable guidelines. In this work, we present $\textbf{M}$ulti-agent $\textbf{IN}$duction and $\textbf{D}$eduction for $\textbf{Skill}$s ($\textbf{MIND-Skill}$), a framework that automatically induces generalizable skills from successful trajectories with robust quality guarantees. MIND-Skill consists of an induction agent which is tasked to abstract reusable skills from successful trajectories, and a deduction agent which aims to reconstruct trajectories by following the induced skills. To guarantee the quality of the generated skills, we introduce a reconstruction loss that compares input and reconstructed trajectories, an outcome loss that enforces the correctness of the reconstructed trajectories, and a rubric loss that assesses the documentation quality and regularizes the abstraction level of the generated skills according to predefined criteria. These textual losses are jointly optimized with TextGrad, and the resulting skills are evaluated on held-out tasks unseen during optimization. Experiments on AppWorld and BFCL-v3 show that MIND-Skill consistently outperforms concurrent skill generation methods.

</details>


### [88] [Reconciling Consistency-Based Diagnosis with Actual-Causality-Based Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08688)
*Leopoldo Bertossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从可解释人工智能（XAI）的角度，探讨了基于一致性的诊断（CBD）与实际因果关系及因果责任之间的联系，指出这些领域间的关联可能对XAI和可解释数据管理产生积极影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示在XAI社区中较少受到关注的基于一致性的诊断（CBD）与其他因果推理方法（如实际因果关系和因果责任）之间的潜在联系，以促进XAI的发展。

Method: 通过理论分析，建立CBD与实际因果关系及因果责任之间的概念联系。

Result: 成功建立了CBD与实际因果关系及因果责任之间的联系，强调了这些联系对XAI和可解释数据管理的潜在贡献。

Conclusion: CBD与因果推理方法之间存在重要联系，进一步研究这些联系有助于推动XAI和可解释数据管理的进步。

Abstract: We establish, from the point of view of Explainable AI (XAI), connections between Consistency-Based Diagnosis (CBD), on one side, and Actual Causality and Causal Responsibility, on the other. CBD has received little attention from the XAI community. Connections between these two areas could have a fruitful impact on XAI and Explainable Data Management.

</details>


### [89] [MBP-KT: Learning Global Collaborative Information from Meta-Behavioral Pattern for Enhanced Knowledge Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08697)
*Yuhao Jia,Duantengchuan Li,Jinsong Chen,Zhongjie Mao,Mingwen Tong,Yue Li,Xiaoguang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种通用的元行为模式感知框架MBP-KT，用于知识追踪，通过构建元行为序列并提取全局协作表示来提升多种下游模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于原始交互序列的知识追踪方法难以深入捕捉学习行为模式且泛化能力有限，需更有效的协作信息利用方式。

Method: 设计元行为序列构造方法，将原始交互转化为元行为模式组合，并开发无参数模块提取全局协作表示，提供通用注入策略以适配多种KT模型。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，MBP-KT能持续提升多种KT模型的性能。

Conclusion: MBP-KT通过挖掘元行为模式和全局协作信息，有效增强了知识追踪模型的表现力和通用性。

Abstract: The emerging collaborative information-based knowledge tracing (KT) has been a promising way to enhance modeling of learners' knowledge states. The core idea is to extract the collaborative information from interaction sequences of other learners to assist the prediction on the target one. Despite effectiveness, existing methods are built on the raw interaction sequences with tailored modules, which inevitably limits their capacity in deeply capturing learning behavioral patterns and generalization. To this end, we propose a general meta-behavioral pattern-aware framework (MBP-KT) for KT. Specifically, MBP-KT introduces a novel meta-behavioral sequence construction to transform the raw interaction sequences into the combinations of different meta-behavioral patterns. In this way, the learning behavioral patterns of learners can be effectively preserved. Then, MBP-KT develops a parameter-free module to extract the global collaborative representations from the constructed meta-behavioral sequences. Moreover, MBP-KT provides general injection strategies to introduce the extracted global collaborative information into various downstream KT models, ensuring the universality of the collaborative information. Extensive results on real-world datasets demonstrate that MBP-KT can consistently boosts the performance of a wide range of KT models.

</details>


### [90] [AgentPSO: Evolving Agent Reasoning Skill via Multi-agent Particle Swarm Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08704)
*Hyunmin Hwang,Jaemin Kim,Choonghan Kim,Hangeol Chang,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgentPSO，一种受粒子群优化启发的多智能体推理框架，通过自然语言技能和语义更新方向迭代提升智能体的推理能力，无需更新语言模型参数即可实现可迁移的推理技能学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体推理方法依赖推理时的辩论或聚合，易受错误影响且智能体推理能力不随任务进化，缺乏对可复用推理技能的持续提升机制。

Method: 将每个智能体视为具有自然语言技能状态和语义更新方向的‘粒子’，结合个体历史最优、群体最优及自反方向，通过类粒子群算法迭代更新其推理技能，从而在不调整语言模型参数的情况下提升推理表现。

Result: 在数学与通用推理基准上，AgentPSO优于静态单智能体与仅测试时多智能体方法，且所学技能可跨基准和不同语言模型迁移，验证了其捕捉通用推理过程的能力。

Conclusion: AgentPSO提供了一种无需参数更新即可持续进化多智能体推理技能的新范式，展现出强大的性能提升与技能可迁移性。

Abstract: Multi-agent reasoning has shown promise for improving the problem-solving ability of large language models by allowing multiple agents to explore diverse reasoning paths. However, most existing multi-agent methods rely on inference-time debate or aggregation, which can be vulnerable to incorrect peer influence and biased consensus. Moreover, the agents themselves remain static, as their underlying reasoning skills do not evolve across tasks. In this paper, we introduce AgentPSO, a particle-swarm-inspired framework for evolving multi-agent reasoning skills. AgentPSO treats each agent as a particle-like reasoner whose state is a natural-language skill and whose velocity is a semantic update direction, iteratively moving agents toward stronger skill states to improve both individual and collective reasoning performance. Across training iterations, each agent updates its skill by combining its previous velocity, personal-best skill, global-best skill, and a self-reflective direction derived from peer reasoning trajectories. This enables agents to learn reusable reasoning behaviors from both their own experiences and the strongest skills discovered by the population, without updating the parameters of the backbone language model. Experiments on mathematical and general reasoning benchmarks show that AgentPSO improves over static single-agent skills and test-time-only multi-agent reasoning baselines. The evolved skills further transfer across benchmarks and to another backbone model, suggesting that AgentPSO captures reusable reasoning procedures rather than merely optimizing benchmark-specific prompts. Code is open-sourced at https://github.com/HYUNMIN-HWANG/AgentPSO/.

</details>


### [91] [When Can Human-AI Teams Outperform Individuals? Tight Bounds with Impossibility Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08710)
*Dongxin Guo,Jikun Wu,Siu-Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于置信度聚合规则的信号检测与信息论结合框架，推导出人类-AI团队互补性的理论边界，解释了为何互补性在现实中罕见，并提供了可操作的设计公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究显示人类-AI团队在70%的情况下无法超越其最佳成员，但缺乏理论说明何时能实现互补性。本文旨在建立一个理论框架来明确这一条件。

Method: 结合信号检测理论与信息论分析，对广泛的基于置信度的聚合规则推导紧致界限，并推广到多类别情形。

Result: 1) 提出互补性定理：当人类与AI的错误相关性ρ_HM < ρ* 时团队表现优于个体；2) 增益规模为Θ(√Δd)，取决于元认知敏感性差异；3) 不可能性结果：当ρ_HM ≥ ρ* 时，任何基于置信度的聚合规则都无法实现互补；4) 多类情况下阈值ρ*_K ≈ ρ*/√(K−1)，并在ImageNet-16H、CIFAR-10H和人类实验数据中验证预测准确性（R达0.91–0.94），且对非高斯分布具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 互补性仅在人类与AI错误相关性足够低时才可实现，该框架解释了其稀有性，并为设计高效人机协作系统提供了理论依据和实用公式。

Abstract: Human-AI teams fail to outperform their best member in 70% of studies, yet no theory specifies when complementarity is achievable. We derive tight bounds for the broad class of confidence-based aggregation rules by integrating signal detection theory with information-theoretic analysis, yielding four results: (1) a complementarity theorem (teams outperform individuals iff error correlation $ρ_{HM} < ρ^*$, with $ρ^* \approx a$ in the symmetric near-chance regime); (2) minimax bounds showing gains scale as $Θ(\sqrt{Δd})$ with metacognitive sensitivity difference; (3) an impossibility result proving no confidence-based aggregation rule achieves complementarity when $ρ_{HM} \geq ρ^*$; and (4) multi-class generalization $ρ^*_K \approx ρ^*/\sqrt{K-1}$. Predictions match observed team accuracy ($R = 0.94$ on ImageNet-16H, $R = 0.91$ on CIFAR-10H) and the multi-class threshold scaling holds on human data ($R = 0.93$, $K = 16$), with robustness under non-Gaussian distributions. The framework explains why complementarity is rare and provides actionable design formulas; results apply to aggregation, not to interactive deliberation that generates novel answers.

</details>


### [92] [Bias by Necessity: Impossibility Theorems for Sequential Processing with Convergent AI and Human Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08716)
*Jikun Wu,Dongxin Guo,Siu-Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明了自回归语言模型中的因果掩码约束导致了认知偏差（如首因效应、锚定效应和顺序依赖）的必然性，并通过三个不可能性定理和人类实验验证了这些理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨某些认知偏差是否是序列信息处理的数学必然结果，揭示认知偏差在人工智能与人类认知中的共通机制。

Method: 提出三个不可能性定理，结合12个前沿大语言模型的实证分析，并在两个预先注册的人类实验中检验理论预测。

Result: 发现首因效应源于不对称注意力累积，锚定效应来自序列条件化的信息边界，且精确去偏需阶乘时间计算；实验验证了锚点位置影响锚定强度，工作记忆负荷增强首因效应。

Conclusion: 认知偏差是序列处理架构下的资源理性响应，而非单纯认知缺陷，为AI与人类认知提供了统一解释框架。

Abstract: Are certain cognitive biases mathematically inevitable consequences of sequential information processing? We prove that primacy effects, anchoring, and order-dependence are architecturally necessary in autoregressive language models due to causal masking constraints. Our three impossibility theorems establish: (1) primacy bias arises from asymmetric attention accumulation; (2) anchoring emerges from sequential conditioning with provable information bounds; and (3) exact debiasing by permutation marginalization requires factorial-time computation, with Monte Carlo approximation feasible at constant per-tolerance overhead. We validate these bounds across 12 frontier LLMs ($R^2 = 0.89$; $Δ$BIC $= 16.6$ vs. next-best alternative). We then derive quantitative predictions from the framework and test them in two pre-registered human experiments ($N = 464$ analyzed). Study 1 confirms anchor position modulates anchoring magnitude ($d = 0.52$, BF$_{10} = 847$). Study 2 shows working memory load amplifies primacy bias ($d = 0.41$, BF$_{10} = 156$), with WM capacity predicting bias reduction ($r = -.38$). These convergent findings reframe cognitive biases as resource-rational responses to sequential processing.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Understanding Asynchronous Inference Methods for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08168)
*Ayoub Agouzoul*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了四种缓解视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型推理延迟导致观测陈旧问题的方法，在统一框架下进行评估，发现A2C2在多数延迟条件下表现最佳，TT-RTC具有良好的鲁棒性，而VLASH存在明显的延迟依赖权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同研究提出了缓解VLA模型异步执行中观测陈旧问题的方法，但因实验设置不一致难以直接比较，缺乏统一评估标准。

Method: 构建两个统一代码库，集成IT-RTC、TT-RTC、VLASH和A2C2四种方法，使用MLPMixer和SmolVLA策略，在Kinetix和LIBERO操纵基准上进行控制变量测试，扫描最高达20步的推理延迟。

Result: A2C2在Kinetix上表现最优（延迟达8步时解决率仍超90%），在LIBERO上从d=4起领先；IT-RTC在低延迟下有效但高延迟下性能骤降；TT-RTC作为训练时方法最稳定且无推理开销；VLASH性能受微调延迟范围明显影响。

Conclusion: A2C2是应对推理延迟最有效的方法，TT-RTC为最稳健的训练时替代方案，VLASH需谨慎选择延迟范围，统一评估揭示了各方法在实际应用中的优劣与适用场景。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising path to generalist robot control, but their inference latency causes observation staleness when generated actions are executed asynchronously. Several methods have been proposed concurrently to mitigate this problem: inference-time inpainting (IT-RTC), training-time delay simulation (TT-RTC), future-state-aware conditioning (VLASH), and lightweight residual correction (A2C2). Each takes a fundamentally different approach, but they have so far been evaluated independently with different codebases, base policies, and protocols. We present a systematic comparison of these four methods under controlled conditions. We develop two unified codebases that integrate all methods with harmonized library and dataset versions, and we benchmark them on the Kinetix suite with MLPMixer policies and on the LIBERO manipulation benchmark with SmolVLA, sweeping inference delays up to $d=20$ control steps. A2C2's per-step residual correction is the most effective method on Kinetix, holding above 90% solve rate up to $d=8$, and also leads on LIBERO from $d=4$ onwards. IT-RTC is competitive at low delays but degrades sharply under long chunks ($H=30$) and high delays. TT-RTC is the most robust training-based method: stable across $d_\max$ choices, generalizes beyond its training delay distribution, and adds zero inference overhead. VLASH exhibits a clear low-delay vs. high-delay trade-off governed by the fine-tuning delay range $[0,d_\max]$. Code is available at https://github.com/TheAyos/async-vla-inference

</details>


### [94] [Anatomical Landmark-Guided Deep Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Gastric Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08269)
*Haoxuan Wu,Sishen Yuan,Haitao Gao,Zhen Li,Xiuli Zuo,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于解剖标志引导的深度强化学习（AL-DRL）框架，用于无线胶囊内窥镜的自主胃部导航，具有高黏膜覆盖率和良好的跨患者解剖结构迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无线胶囊内窥镜导航方法在不同患者解剖结构间迁移性差，且难以实现完整的黏膜覆盖，限制了其诊断潜力。

Method: 设计了一种轻量级边缘轮廓-深度融合模块，利用稳定的低维解剖标志坐标作为输入，结合深度强化学习进行导航决策，并采用两阶段仿真到现实的迁移策略与自适应动态规划控制器以应对物理扰动。

Result: 在八个患者衍生模型的仿真中，50秒内实现超过97%的覆盖；离体实验达到平均87%的覆盖率，并比专家手动操作减少53%的操作时间。

Conclusion: 所提出的AL-DRL框架显著提升了无线胶囊内窥镜在胃部的自主导航性能，具备良好的临床应用前景。

Abstract: Wireless capsule endoscopy (WCE) enables painless visualization of the gastrointestinal tract, but its diagnostic potential is limited by incomplete mucosal coverage and poor transferability of existing navigation methods across patient anatomies. We propose a transferable, anatomical landmarkguided deep reinforcement learning (AL-DRL) framework for autonomous gastric navigation. Leveraging a lightweight edgecontour-depth fusion module, our policy operates on stable, lowdimensional landmark coordinates rather than high-dimensional video streams, effectively bridging the sim-to-real gap. In simulations across eight patient-derived models, the method achieves over 97% coverage within 50 seconds, significantly outperforming vanilla PPO, SAC, and DQN agents. A two-stage sim-to-real pipeline with an adaptive dynamic programming controller actively mitigates physical disturbances. Ex-vivo experiments demonstrate a mean coverage of 87% and a 53% reduction in procedure time compared with expert manual control.

</details>


### [95] [LE-PAVD: Learning-Enhanced Physics-Aware Vehicle Dynamics for High-Speed Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08489)
*Musabbir Ahmed Arrafi,Malik Ali,Nicholas M. Stiffler,Krishna Bhavithavya Kidambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LE-PAVD的混合车辆动力学模型，结合物理先验与学习组件，在保持物理一致性的同时显著提升了状态预测精度和闭环控制性能，并降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确建模非线性车辆动力学对高速自动驾驶赛车至关重要，现有模型在物理一致性和泛化能力之间存在权衡。

Method: 提出LE-PAVD模型，融合物理先验与可学习模块，引入四种增强组件：负载敏感的Pacejka轮胎力、纵向载荷转移、横向轮胎力效应和速率受限的执行器输入，并在仿真和实测数据上端到端训练。

Result: 在未见赛道上，相比纯学习基线，平均位移误差降低16.1%，最终位移误差降低20.6%，横摆角速度RMSE降低91.3%，且FLOPs减少21.6%，推理速度快1.5倍；闭环实验中训练赛道和测试赛道圈速分别提升17.4%和9.5%，无边界违规。

Conclusion: LE-PAVD提供了一个紧凑、基于物理的动力学框架，兼顾预测精度、泛化能力和计算效率，适用于极限工况下的自动驾驶。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of nonlinear vehicle dynamics is essential for high-speed autonomous racing, where controllers operate at the handling limits. Model-based methods are interpretable but rely on simplifying assumptions, while purely learned models capture nonlinearities yet often lack physical consistency and generalization. We propose LE-PAVD (Learning-Enhanced Physics-Aware Vehicle Dynamics), a hybrid model that integrates physics priors with learned components. Our architecture adds four components: load-sensitive Pacejka tire forces, longitudinal load transfer, lateral tire-force effects, and rate-limited actuator inputs. Trained end-to-end on simulation and real-world telemetry, LE-PAVD enforces physical consistency while improving state prediction accuracy. On an unseen track, LE-PAVD reduces average displacement error (ADE) by 16.1$\%$, final displacement error (FDE) by 20.6$\%$, and lowers yaw-rate root mean squared error (RMSE) by 91.3$\%$ versus a deep dynamics baseline, while using 21.6$\%$ fewer FLOPs and achieving approximately 1.50$\times$ faster inference. In closed-loop simulations, LE-PAVD consistently outperforms the baseline by achieving faster lap times by 17.4$\%$ on a training track and 9.5$\%$ on a test track, without any track boundary violations. Overall, LE-PAVD offers a compact, physics-grounded dynamics backbone that improves predictive fidelity and closed-loop performance while reducing inference cost.

</details>


### [96] [Trajectory-Consistent Flow Matching for Robust Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08511)
*Riad Ahmed,Sujosh Nag,Moniruzzaman Akash,Mostafa Hussein,Momotaz Begum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的流匹配策略，通过四种互补方法解决训练与推理之间的不匹配问题，显著提升了机器人操作任务中的轨迹准确性和执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流匹配策略在训练时优化逐点速度目标，而推理需要对速度场进行数值积分，导致累积轨迹误差。本文旨在弥合这一训练-推理差距。

Method: 提出四种互补方法：(1) 辅助修正流速度回归，提供全时间区间上的均匀监督；(2) 多步轨迹一致性训练，直接监督速度场在轨迹段上的积分位移；(3) 速度场正则化，增强时间平滑性以稳定积分；(4) 使用四阶龙格-库塔（RK4）进行推理。同时采用双视图3D点云编码器提升空间感知。

Result: 在四个真实机器人任务中，本文方法在两个长视野多阶段任务上分别达到70%和60%的成功率（基线为0%），并在精密工具放置任务上达到100%成功率。三个MetaWorld仿真任务也验证了性能的一致提升。

Conclusion: 轨迹级监督和高阶积分方法对可靠策略执行至关重要，所提组件需协同工作才能有效减少推理轨迹漂移和误差。

Abstract: Flow matching policies learn continuous velocity fields that transport noise to actions, enabling fast deterministic inference for robot manipulation. However, standard training optimizes a pointwise velocity objective while inference requires numerical integration of that field -- a mismatch that causes compounding trajectory errors. We propose four complementary remedies: (1) auxiliary rectified flow velocity regression that provides uniform temporal supervision across the full time interval; (2) multi-step trajectory consistency training that supervises the integrated displacement of the velocity field over trajectory segments, directly closing the train-inference gap; (3) velocity field regularization that enforces temporal smoothness, preventing oscillations that destabilize integration; and (4) fourth-order Runge-Kutta (RK4) inference that reduces global discretization error by orders of magnitude over Euler methods. Critically, these components are not independently sufficient -- RK4 without a smooth velocity field fails, and smoothness without trajectory-level supervision still drifts, as our ablation study confirms. We further pair these with a dual-view 3D point cloud encoder using two independent PointNet encoders for complementary spatial perception. On four real-robot tasks across a Franka arm and a Boston Dynamics Spot, our method achieves 70% and 60% overall success on two long-horizon multi-phase tasks where both baselines score 0%, and reaches 100% on precision tool placement. Three MetaWorld simulation tasks confirm consistent improvements, validating that trajectory-level supervision is essential for reliable policy execution.

</details>


### [97] [Model-Reference Adaptive Flight Control of the 95-mg Bee++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08525)
*Francisco M. F. R. Gonçalves,Conor K. Trygstad,Néstor O. Pérez-Arancibia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于微型飞行器Bee++的高性能位置跟踪的模型参考自适应控制架构，并通过实时飞行实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现对微型飞行器Bee++在复杂环境中高精度的位置跟踪控制，解决传统控制方法难以应对系统不确定性与外部干扰的问题。

Method: 采用模型参考自适应控制（MRAC）架构，通过设计参考模型和自适应律，实时调整控制器参数以应对系统动态变化。

Result: 在真实飞行实验中实现了高性能的位置跟踪，验证了所提方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的MRAC架构能够有效提升Bee++在实际飞行中的位置跟踪性能，适用于具有高度不确定性的微型飞行器控制系统。

Abstract: We introduce a model-reference adaptive control (MRAC) architecture for high-performance positional tracking of the Bee++, a 95-mg insect-scale flapping-wing aerial vehicle. The suitability, functionality, and high performance of the proposed approach are demonstrated using data from real-time flight experiments.

</details>


### [98] [BEACON: Cross-Domain Co-Training of Generative Robot Policies via Best-Effort Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08571)
*Antong Zhang,Han Qi,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BEACON是一种面向跨域协同训练的生成式机器人策略训练框架，通过差异感知的重要性重加权方法，结合扩散模型与实例级权重学习，在目标域泛化能力指导下实现源域与目标域的高效适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在源域演示丰富而目标域演示有限的情况下，如何有效进行跨域迁移并提升目标域上的策略泛化能力是一个关键挑战。现有方法如固定比例协同训练或特征对齐可能无法充分适应域间差异。

Method: 将跨域协同训练建模为差异感知的重要性重加权问题，联合学习基于扩散的视觉运动策略和每个样本的源域权重；提出可扩展的实例级差异估计器、随机交替更新机制，并支持多源域平衡。

Result: 在仿真到仿真、仿真到真实及多源域操作任务中，BEACON在鲁棒性和数据效率上优于仅使用目标数据、固定比例协同训练和特征对齐基线方法；即使没有显式对齐目标，也能隐式实现特征对齐。

Conclusion: BEACON通过理论驱动的差异感知重加权机制，实现了高效实用的跨域策略适应，为少样本目标环境下机器人策略训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce BEACON--Best-Effort Adaptation for Cross-Domain Co-Training--a theory-driven framework for training generative robot policies with abundant source demonstrations and limited target demonstrations. BEACON casts cross-domain co-training as a discrepancy-aware importance-reweighting problem, jointly learning a diffusion-based visuomotor policy and per-sample source weights that minimize an objective informed by target-domain generalization guarantees. To make best-effort adaptation practical for high-dimensional sequence policies, we develop scalable instance-level discrepancy estimators, stochastic alternating updates for policy and weights, and a multi-source extension that balances heterogeneous source domains. Across sim-to-sim, sim-to-real, and multi-source manipulation settings, BEACON improves robustness and data efficiency over target-only, fixed-ratio co-training, and feature-alignment baselines. Importantly, even without an explicit alignment objective, BEACON achieves feature alignment as an implicit result of discrepancy-aware cross-domain co-training.

</details>


### [99] [ATAAT: Adaptive Threat-Aware Adversarial Tuning Framework against Backdoor Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08612)
*Kewei Chen,Yayu Long,Shuai Li,Mingsheng Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种针对视觉语言动作（VLA）模型视觉通道的后门攻击新框架ATAAT，解决了传统攻击因梯度干扰导致优化失败的问题，实现了高成功率、低投毒率且隐蔽性强的攻击，揭示了VLA模型的关键安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于传统后门攻击在VLA模型上常因端到端训练中的策略冲突导致优化失败（即“梯度干扰”），难以有效实施，因此需要一种新的攻击范式来揭示其潜在安全风险。

Method: 提出了自适应威胁感知对抗调优（ATAAT）框架，核心是“威胁-方法自适应映射”机制，根据攻击者能力智能选择最优的梯度解耦策略，从而克服梯度干扰问题。

Result: 实验表明，ATAAT在仅5%投毒率下实现了超过80%的目标攻击成功率（TASR），首次实现了对数据投毒场景下的语义级触发器进行隐式解耦攻击，具备高效性和强隐蔽性。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了VLA模型在视觉路径上的关键安全漏洞，为后续防御机制的设计提供了理论与方法支持。

Abstract: Addressing the escalating security vulnerabilities in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, this study investigates backdoor attacks targeting the visual pathway. We identify a core obstacle causing the failure of traditional attack paradigms: "Gradient Interference." This phenomenon represents an optimization failure triggered by conflicting strategies during end-to-end training. To resolve this, we propose an Adaptive Threat-Aware Adversarial Tuning (ATAAT) framework. Through its core "Threat-Method Adaptive Mapping" mechanism, ATAAT intelligently selects the optimal gradient decoupling strategy based on the adversary's capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ATAAT exhibits significant advantages, achieving a highly robust Targeted Attack Success Rate (TASR > 80%) while maintaining extreme stealthiness with merely a 5% poisoning rate. It efficiently handles complex semantic-level triggers and achieves implicit decoupled attacks in data poisoning scenarios for the first time. This work reveals a critical security vulnerability in VLAs and provides theoretical and methodological support for future defense architectures.

</details>


### [100] [Geometry Guided Self-Consistency for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08638)
*Yinwei Dai,Zhuofu Chen,Lijie Yang,Ravi Netravali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KeyStone是一种用于基于扩散的动作生成的推理时自洽方法，通过并行采样多个候选动作块、在连续动作空间中聚类，并返回最大簇的中心点，从而提升任务成功率，且几乎不增加延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理AI模型在每轮推理中生成单一动作轨迹，由于推理过程本身具有随机性，这种单一轨迹策略在多轮序列决策中容易变得脆弱，导致性能下降。

Method: 提出KeyStone方法：并行从共享模型上下文中采样K个候选动作块，在连续动作空间中进行聚类，并选择最大簇的中位点（medoid）作为输出；利用动作轨迹的几何结构特性，使欧氏距离能反映物理相似性，从而实现无需额外训练判别器的选择机制。

Result: 在多种视觉-语言-动作模型（VLAs）和世界-动作模型（WAMs）上验证，KeyStone相比单轨迹采样将任务成功率最高提升13.3%，延迟开销可忽略，并且在无训练成本的情况下达到与基于模型的选择器相当的精度。

Conclusion: KeyStone通过引入推理时的自洽机制，在不增加训练成本和显著计算开销的前提下，有效提升了扩散模型生成动作的鲁棒性和任务成功率，适用于需要高可靠性的序列决策场景。

Abstract: State-of-the-art physical AI models generate a chunk of actions per inference through diffusion or flow matching, iteratively refining an initial noise sample into an action trajectory. Because this inference process is inherently stochastic, committing to a single trajectory per round is brittle, and this brittleness compounds across the many sequential rounds that comprise a complete episode. We introduce KeyStone, an inference-time self-consistency method for diffusion-based action generation that draws $K$ candidate action chunks in parallel from a shared model context, clusters them in continuous action space, and returns the medoid of the largest cluster -- no additional model required. Two properties make this practical. First, the compact nature of action trajectories makes diffusion inference memory-bandwidth bound, leaving spare compute capacity to run $K$ chains in parallel with no additional wall-clock latency. Second, unlike token or pixel spaces where distance carries no semantic meaning and selection requires a learned judge, action chunks are geometrically structured such that Euclidean distance directly reflects physical similarity, making selection principled and judge-free. Across diverse vision-language-action models (VLAs) and world-action models (WAMs), KeyStone improves task success rates by up to \textbf{13.3\%} over single-trajectory sampling with negligible latency overhead, while having on par accuracy with model-based selectors at no training cost. We open source KeyStone at https://github.com/dywsjtu/keystone.

</details>


### [101] [REAP: Reinforcement-Learning End-to-End Autonomous Parking with Gaussian Splatting Simulator for Real2Sim2Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08713)
*Changze Li,Zhe Chen,Shaoyu Chen,Lisen Mu,Yijian Li,Yuelong Yu,Qian Zhang,Qing Su,Ming Yang,Tong Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的端到端自动泊车方法REAP，结合非对称强化学习框架和行为克隆，提升了训练效率与实际部署性能，并通过Real2Sim2Real仿真系统实现真实场景迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多阶段泊车方法在极端场景下表现不佳，且现有端到端方法在数据成本、探索效率等方面存在局限，需一种高效且可实际部署的端到端强化学习泊车方案。

Method: 采用Soft Actor-Critic算法构建非对称强化学习框架，结合规则规划器的行为克隆进行知识蒸馏，并引入软预测碰撞惩罚机制；利用3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）构建Real2Sim2Real仿真系统以实现真实世界迁移。

Result: REAP在多种泊车场景中成功实现泊车，尤其在极窄机械车位等极端情况下表现出色，显著降低碰撞率并加快模型收敛。

Conclusion: REAP实现了高效训练与真实车辆上的直接部署，验证了端到端强化学习在复杂自动泊车任务中的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: In recent years, autonomous parking has made significant advances, yet parking tasks still face challenges in extreme scenarios such as mechanical and dead-end parking slots, often resulting in failures. This is mainly due to traditional parking methods adopting a multistage approach, lacking the ability to optimize the parking problem as a whole. End-to-end methods enable joint optimization across perception and planning modules to eliminate the accumulation of errors, enhancing algorithm performance in extreme scenarios. Although several end-to-end parking methods use imitation or reinforcement learning, the former is limited by data cost and distribution coverage, while the latter suffers from inefficient exploration. To address these challenges, we propose a Reinforcement learning End-to-end Autonomous Parking method (REAP). REAP employs Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) within an asymmetric reinforcement learning framework to improve training efficiency and inference performance. To accelerate model convergence, we distill the capabilities of a rule-based planner into the end-to-end network through behavior cloning. We further introduce a soft predictive collision penalty mechanism to reduce collision rates by penalizing obstacle-approaching actions. To ensure that the trained reinforcement learning network can directly transfer to real-world scenarios, we have established a Real2Sim2Real simulator. In the Real2Sim step, we use 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to transform real-world scenes into digital scenes. In the Sim2Real step, we deploy the end-to-end model onto the vehicle to bridge the Sim2Real gap. Trained in the 3DGS simulator and deployed on physical vehicles, REAP successfully parks in various types of parking spaces, especially demonstrating the feasibility of end-to-end RL parking in extremely narrow mechanical slots.

</details>


### [102] [HULK: Large-scale Hierarchical Coordination under Continual and Uncertain Temporal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08722)
*Qingyuan Luo,Jie Li,Meng Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HULK的分层框架，用于解决在持续且不确定的时间任务下大规模多智能体系统的协调问题，通过滚动分配和动态协调提升计算效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常假设任务是静态且预先已知的，难以应对实际应用中不断在线生成、子任务数量不确定的任务需求，导致需要频繁全局重计算与通信，效率低下。

Method: 提出HULK分层框架，包含两个交错层次：在一定时间范围内将已知任务滚动分配给子团队，以及在子团队内根据执行过程中检测到的子任务进行动态协调。任务以协作动作为基础的时间逻辑公式表示。

Result: 该方法在大规模异构系统中进行了严格验证，能够在多种时间任务和环境不确定性下有效运行，显著提高计算效率和系统鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HULK通过在不同粒度和触发条件下进行分层协调，能够高效应对持续生成且具有不确定性的任务，适用于现实中的复杂多智能体协作场景。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems can be extremely efficient when working concurrently and collaboratively, e.g., for delivery, surveillance, search and rescue. Coordination of such teams often involves two aspects: selecting appropriate subteams for different tasks in various areas, and coordinating agents in the subteams to execute the associated subtasks. Existing work often assumes that the tasks are static and known beforehand, where an integer program can be formulated and solved offline. However, in many applications, the team-wise tasks are generated online continually by external requests, and the amount of subtasks within each task is uncertain, e.g., the number of packages to deliver or victims to rescue. The aforementioned offline solution becomes inadequate as it would require constant re-computation for the whole team and global communication to broadcast the results. Thus, this work tackles the large-scale coordination problem under continual and uncertain temporal tasks, specified as temporal logic formulas over collaborative actions. The proposed hierarchical framework, HULK, consists of two interleaved layers: the rolling assignment of currently known tasks to subteams within a certain horizon, and the dynamic coordination within a subteam given the detected subtasks during online execution. Thus, coordination is performed hierarchically at different granularities and triggering conditions, improving computational efficiency and robustness. The method is validated rigorously over large-scale heterogeneous systems under various temporal tasks and environment uncertainties.

</details>


### [103] [Latent Geometry Beyond Search: Amortizing Planning in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08732)
*Hoang Nguyen,Xiaohao Xu,Xiaonan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于结构化潜在空间的轻量级目标条件逆动力学模型（GC-IDM），通过将规划负担从在线搜索转移到学习推理，在多个环境中实现了与传统优化方法相当甚至更优的控制性能，同时显著降低了决策成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在预训练的世界模型中，何时学习到的表示能够真正简化控制而不仅仅是预测，探索潜在空间几何特性对规划效率的影响。

Method: 利用具有平滑性和均匀性正则化的LeWorldModel的潜在几何结构，设计了一个轻量级的目标条件逆动力学模型（GC-IDM），直接从当前状态、目标状态和剩余时间步映射到下一个动作，替代迭代规划方法。

Result: 在四个基准环境（包括导航、接触丰富操作和连续控制）中的实验表明，GC-IDM在八项设置中的七项上表现优于或等于CEM，并且每次决策的成本降低了100-130倍；进一步测试多种在线规划器表明结果具有普适性。

Conclusion: 结构良好的潜在空间可以将部分规划任务从在线优化转移至学习推断，表明学习到的表示本身已编码了大量可用于高效控制的结构信息。

Abstract: Modern vision-based world models can represent observations as compact yet expressive latent manifolds, but fast goal-oriented planning in these spaces remains challenging. This raises a central question: when does a learned representation simplify control, rather than merely enabling prediction? We study this question in a pretrained LeWorldModel, whose latent geometry is regularized for smoothness and uniformity. Our key insight is that, under such geometry, planning can be amortized into a latent inverse-dynamics mapping instead of requiring online search. We therefore replace iterative planning with a lightweight Goal-Conditioned Inverse Dynamics Model (GC-IDM) that maps the current latent state, goal latent state, and remaining horizon directly to the next action. Empirically, across four benchmark environments spanning navigation, contact-rich manipulation, and continuous control, our controller matches or exceeds CEM in seven of eight environment-protocol settings while reducing per-decision cost by 100-130x. A broader sweep over test-time planners (CEM, MPPI, iCEM, and gradient-based methods) shows that this result is not specific to a particular optimizer. These findings suggest that much of the structure recovered by test-time planning is already locally encoded in the latent representation. More broadly, our results indicate that sufficiently structured latent spaces can shift part of the planning burden from online optimization to learned inference.

</details>


### [104] [A Visuo-Tactile Data Collection System with Haptic Feedback for Coarse-to-Fine Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08757)
*Yeseung Kim,Nayoung Oh,Jun Park,Teetat Thamronglak,Daehyung Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种结合视觉和触觉的时序结构化数据采集系统，用于生成高质量的接触丰富的模仿学习示范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统系统常将操作者与接触力解耦，难以表现细微的力控制，限制了复杂操作任务的示范质量。

Method: 开发一种直接驱动的夹爪装置，由操作者手指直接操控以保留自然的触觉反馈；集成视觉传感器和定制触觉阵列，并通过手柄按钮实时标注任务关键时序区域。

Result: 实现了触觉、视觉与力感知的多模态数据同步采集，并支持任务时序结构的实时标注，生成适用于粗到精学习算法的高质量数据集。

Conclusion: 该系统通过融合触觉反馈与实时时序标注，提升了模仿学习中操作策略的学习效果，适用于复杂接触丰富的机器人操作任务。

Abstract: We present a visuo-tactile data-collection system that generates temporally structured, contact-rich demonstrations for imitation learning. Conventional systems often decouple the operator from contact forces, which hinders the demonstration of subtle force modulation. Our system introduces a direct-drive gripper that the operator actuates with the fingers, preserving natural haptic feedback. Integrated visual sensors and custom tactile arrays capture image streams and contact geometry. A handle-mounted push button enables the operator to annotate the task's temporal structure in real time by marking task-critical regions. By fusing in-hand force perception with in-situ temporal annotation, the system produces multimodal datasets designed for coarse-to-fine learning algorithms that exploit structural task knowledge, enabling the development of high-quality manipulation policies.

</details>


### [105] [Omni-scale Learning-based Sequential Decision Framework for Order Fulfillment of Tote-handling Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08758)
*Jiaxin Liu,Peng Yang,Yuping Li,Xinyue Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种通用且可扩展的基于学习的顺序决策框架OLSF-TRS，用于托盘搬运机器人系统的订单履行，结合组合优化与多智能体强化学习，在小规模系统中接近最优，大规模下显著减少托盘移动并提升实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电商和小批量生产的发展，料箱逐渐取代托盘成为主要物流单元，现有订单履行决策方法缺乏通用性和可迁移性，难以适应不同系统配置。

Method: 提出OLSF-TRS框架，融合结构化组合优化与多智能体强化学习，统一协调订单、料箱与机器人的顺序决策，支持从小规模到大规模系统的泛化应用。

Result: 在小规模系统中平均最优性差距低于3.5%；在大规模场景下相比启发式基线减少8-12%的总料箱移动，相比最先进基于规则的方法减少超过30%，并保持实时响应能力。

Conclusion: OLSF-TRS为料箱搬运机器人系统提供了高效、统一的订单履行决策框架，具备良好的通用性与扩展性，可显著降低运营成本与能耗，提升吞吐稳定性，适用于电商与工业物流场景。

Abstract: Driven by the rapid expansion of e-commerce and small-batch production, the size of the intralogistics load unit of finished goods, semi-finished goods and raw materials is steadily shrinking. Totes are gradually replacing pallets as the primary handling and storage container. This shift has propelled tote-handling robotic systems to the forefront of automation order fulfillment centers. The order-fulfillment decisions of tote-handling robotic systems share a common order-tote-robot sequential decision-making nature. Existing studies primarily focus on decision mechanisms tailored to particular systems, making it difficult to generalize or transfer them to other contexts. We propose an Omni-scale Learning-based Sequential Decision Framework for Order Fulfillment of Tote-handling Robotic Systems (OLSF-TRS), a generalized and scalable sequential decision framework that combines structured combinatorial optimization with multi-agent reinforcement learning to coordinate order,tote, and robot decisions. On small-scale tote-handling robotic systems, OLSF-TRS achieves near-optimal performance with average optimality gaps below 3.5% across two distinct system configurations. In large-scale scenarios, OLSF-TRS consistently outperforms heuristic baselines across two different system types, reducing total tote movements by 8-12% and over 30% compared to SOTA rule-based approaches, while maintaining real-time responsiveness. These improvements translate into tangible operational benefits, including cost reduction, lower energy consumption, and enhanced throughput stability. The proposed framework delivers an efficient and unified order fulfillment decision-making framework for widely deployed tote-handling robotic systems,supporting high-quality order fulfillment in both e-commerce and industrial logistics sectors.

</details>


### [106] [ProcVLM: Learning Procedure-Grounded Progress Rewards for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08774)
*Youhe Feng,Hansen Shi,Haoyang Li,Xinlei Guo,Yang Wang,Chengyang Zhang,Jinkai Zhang,Xiaohan Zhang,Jie Tang,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ProcVLM，一种基于视觉-语言模型的进度感知奖励模型，通过程序结构和阶段内视觉变化来学习密集的、以过程为中心的任务进展信号，克服了传统方法依赖终点成功标签或时间插值的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励模型依赖轨迹级成功标签或时间插值，难以准确反映任务在执行过程中的真实进展，无法捕捉未完成步骤、停滞和失败状态，因此需要一种能理解操作任务程序结构的密集反馈机制。

Method: 提出ProcVLM，采用“先推理后估计”范式，通过推断剩余原子动作来估算任务进度；构建帧级子任务语义标注，依据子任务结构分配进度预算，并结合子任务内的视觉变化进行进度分布；建立标准化的程序监督合成流程，利用30个具身数据集构建包含6000万标注帧的ProcCorpus-60M，并从中生成用于预训练的ProcVQA数据集。

Result: 实验表明，ProcVLM在ProcVQA和奖励模型基准上优于代表性基线，在具身程序推理和轨迹内部进度区分能力方面表现更优，可作为下游任务中奖励引导策略优化的有效密集奖励模型。

Conclusion: ProcVLM通过将进度估计扎根于程序结构和视觉变化，并引入推理前置机制，实现了对长视界机器人操作任务更精细、准确的进度建模，为密集奖励学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation requires dense feedback that reflects how a task advances through its procedural stages, not merely whether the final outcome is successful. Existing reward models often rely on trajectory-level success labels or time-based interpolation, which can conflate elapsed time with true task progress and therefore fail to capture unfinished steps, stagnation, and failure states. We present ProcVLM, a progress-aware vision-language model that learns procedure-grounded progress as a dense reward signal for manipulation. Rather than deriving progress from terminal outcomes or temporal proxies, ProcVLM grounds progress estimation in procedural structure and intra-stage visual change, and further adopts a reasoning-before-estimation paradigm that infers the remaining atomic actions before estimating task progress. Specifically, we construct this supervision by synthesizing frame-level subtask-semantic annotations, assigning progress budgets according to subtask structure, and distributing each budget based on intra-subtask visual change. To train ProcVLM at scale, we build a standardized procedural supervision synthesis pipeline and construct ProcCorpus-60M from 30 embodied datasets with 60M annotated frames, from which we derive ProcVQA for procedure-aware pretraining, with progress estimation as the central task alongside action segmentation and future planning. Experiments on ProcVQA and reward-model benchmarks show that ProcVLM improves embodied procedural reasoning and yields more discriminative trajectory-internal progress estimates than representative baselines, supporting its use as a dense reward model for downstream reward-guided policy optimization. Project page: https://procvlm.github.io/

</details>


### [107] [ElasticFlow: One-Step Physics-Consistent Policy with Elastic Time Horizons for Language-Guided Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08799)
*Kewei Chen,Yayu Long,Shuai Li,Mingsheng Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ElasticFlow的新型扩散策略框架，无需知识蒸馏即可实现物理一致的单步策略，通过重构平均场理论和引入弹性时间范围机制，在多个基准上实现了高效且高性能的长周期控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散策略在具身AI中表现优异，但其迭代去噪过程导致高延迟，现有加速方法常牺牲物理一致性，因此需要一种既高效又保持物理一致性的新方法。

Method: 提出ElasticFlow框架：1）通过直接建模平均速度场重构平均场理论，实现从噪声到动作的单步映射；2）引入弹性时间范围机制，显式编码控制粒度以克服频谱偏差，对齐语义指令与物理执行周期。

Result: 在LIBERO、CALVIN和RoboTwin等基准上，ElasticFlow实现了约71Hz的高效1-NFE推理，并在长周期任务上优于OpenVLA和$π_0$等最先进方法。

Conclusion: ElasticFlow是一种高效、鲁棒且语义对齐的单步策略框架，解决了扩散模型在机器人控制中的延迟与物理一致性矛盾，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion policies have demonstrated exceptional performance in embodied AI. However, their iterative denoising process results in high latency, and existing acceleration methods often sacrifice physical consistency. To address this, we propose ElasticFlow, a distillation-free, physics-consistent one-step policy framework. We reconstruct the Mean Field Theory by directly modeling the average velocity field, enabling a direct single-step mapping from noise to action. Addressing the Temporal Heterogeneity of robotic tasks, we introduce the Elastic Time Horizons mechanism. This mechanism effectively overcomes Spectral Bias by explicitly encoding control granularity, achieving efficient alignment between semantic instructions and physical execution horizons. Experiments on benchmarks such as LIBERO, CALVIN, and RoboTwin demonstrate that ElasticFlow achieves efficient 1-NFE inference (approximately 71Hz). Furthermore, it outperforms state-of-the-art methods, including OpenVLA and $π_0$, on long-horizon tasks, highlighting its potential for efficient, robust, and semantically aligned control.

</details>


### [108] [Constraint-Aware Diffusion Priors for High-Fidelity and Versatile Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08804)
*Jianhui Chen,Ruixin Zhan,Liu Liu,Yang Cai,Ziqiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Diff-CAST，一种基于扩散模型的约束感知对称跟踪框架，用于解决仿生四足运动中传统GAN判别器的模式崩溃和多源数据扩展问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GAN的判别器易发生模式崩溃，且传统运动先验在复杂动作中存在分布外跟踪冲突和执行器安全风险，限制了大规模多源数据下的应用。

Method: 采用扩散模型建模多模态运动分布以替代GAN判别器，提出Symmetric Augmented Command Conditioning（SACC）实现无漂移跟踪，并结合约束强化学习保障硬件安全。

Result: 实验表明Diff-CAST有效缓解模式崩溃，支持多样化技能间的平滑切换，在四足机器人上实现高保真、抗漂移且符合硬件约束的运动控制。

Conclusion: Diff-CAST通过扩散模型与约束感知架构，提升了模仿学习在复杂、真实场景下的可扩展性与部署安全性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning combined with imitation learning has significantly advanced biomimetic quadrupedal locomotion. However, scaling these frameworks to massive, multi-source datasets exposes fundamental bottlenecks. First, traditional GAN-based discriminators are prone to mode collapse, struggling to capture diverse motion distributions from uncurated datasets. Second, existing kinematic priors suffer from out-of-distribution (OOD) tracking conflicts, leading to severe unintended heading drifts during complex maneuvers. Furthermore, deploying unconstrained priors to physical hardware poses critical safety risks by disregarding actuator dynamics. To overcome these challenges, we propose Diff-CAST (Diffusion-guided Constraint-Aware Symmetric Tracking), a novel motion prior framework leveraging the multi-modal distribution modeling capabilities of diffusion models for stylistic rewards. Diff-CAST effectively replaces traditional GAN discriminators, unlocking robust data scaling on heterogeneous collections. To ensure high-fidelity intent execution and reliable real-world deployment, we introduce a comprehensive Sim2Re architecture integrating Symmetric Augmented Command Conditioning (SACC) for drift-free tracking, and Constrained RL for hardware safety. Experiments on a quadruped demonstrate that Diff-CAST mitigates mode collapse, enables seamless transitions between diverse skills, and ensures robust, hardware-compliant locomotion.

</details>


### [109] [AssemPlanner: A Multi-Agent Based Task Planning Framework for Flexible Assembly System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08831)
*Chenhao Zhang,Chaoran Zhang,Zhaobo Xu,Yongbo Yang,Pingfa Feng,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多智能体的装配任务规划框架AssemPlanner，能够将自然语言描述的任务自动转化为可执行的生产操作序列，提升柔性装配系统的自动化与智能化水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务规划方法在配置新产品的生产线时耗时且依赖多位专家，难以满足柔性装配系统对快速部署的需求。

Method: 设计了一个包含SchedAgent、KnowledgeAgent、LineBalanceAgent和场景图的多智能体框架；SchedAgent作为核心推理引擎，采用ReAct机制根据其他模块反馈动态调整决策，实现对复杂工业约束的自主求解。

Result: 框架能有效将自然语言任务转化为可行的操作序列，并通过多智能体协作实现自适应调度与产线平衡，提升了系统灵活性和部署效率。

Conclusion: AssemPlanner减少了对人工专家配置的依赖，为柔性装配系统提供了一种高效、可复现的自动化任务规划解决方案。

Abstract: In flexible assembly systems, existing task planning methods require a time-consuming configuration process by multiple experts to establish a production line for a new product. To address this challenge, we propose a multi-agent based task planning framework for flexible assembly systems, denoted as AssemPlanner. It takes tasks described in natural language as input, which are then converted into actionable sequential production operations. It comprises several specialized agents, including SchedAgent , KnowledgeAgent, LineBalanceAgent, and a scene graph. Within the proposed framework, SchedAgent serves as the central reasoning engine. Departing from traditional static pipelines, AssemPlanner utilizes a ReAct-based SchedAgent to adaptively adjust actions via multi-agent feedback. By observing the feedback from KnowledgeAgent, LineBalanceAgent, and the scene graph, it autonomously resolves complex industrial process constraints. To facilitate reproducibility, all code and datasets are released at https://github.com/chz332/Assemplanner.

</details>


### [110] [Raymoval: Raycasting-based Dynamic Object Removal for Static 3D Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08937)
*Daebeom Kim,Seungjae Lee,Seoyeon Jang,Kevin Christiansen Marsim,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于raycasting的模块，用于在静态3D建图中去除动态物体，提升地图一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态物体在静态建图中留下残余痕迹并导致表面丢失，降低地图一致性。

Method: 将每帧扫描投影到方位-仰角网格，逐bin比较最小测距与地图首次命中距离，并通过raycast一致性检验区分动态与静态点，最后进行空间一致性验证优化标签。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和自定义数据集上进行了定量与定性评估，结果表明所提方法能生成动态残余更少、过删除更少的静态地图。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了长期自主导航中静态地图的 consistency 和质量。

Abstract: Static mapping is fundamental to robot navigation, providing a persistent geometric prior and a consistent reference for long-term autonomy. However, dynamic objects leave residual traces and cause surface loss, which reduces map consistency. We propose a raycasting-based module for dynamic object removal in static 3D mapping. Each scan is projected onto an azimuth-elevation grid, and for every viewing direction we compare the bin-wise minimum range with the map's first-hit distance computed by raycasting. Furthermore, we apply a raycast consistency test that separates dynamic from static points. Finally, a spatial consistency validation step refines labels, producing static maps with lower residual dynamics and reduced over-removal. We evaluate our approach quantitatively and qualitatively on SemanticKITTI and a challenging custom dataset, and show consistent static mapping results.

</details>


### [111] [A low-cost mockup to simulate robotic laser cutting in nuclear decommissioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08947)
*Frederico Fernandes Afonso Silva,Murilo Marques Marinho,Bruno Vilhena Adorno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本实验模拟装置，用于核退役中容器激光切割过程的仿真，通过自适应运动控制器实现高精度轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了降低核退役过程中激光切割容器的实验成本，并解决系统未校准和碰撞避免的问题。

Method: 采用三轴平台、六自由度机械臂和基于相机与基准标记的视觉系统，结合约束任务空间自适应运动控制方法，控制紫外光束而非末端执行器全姿态。

Result: 实验结果显示，在未校准系统下，控制末端执行器姿态时平均跟踪精度为3.9 mm（标准差2.5 mm），控制紫外光束时提高到2.4 mm（标准差1.3 mm）。

Conclusion: 所提出的系统无需标定且能自主避障，通过控制四自由度紫外光束显著提升了激光切割路径的跟踪精度，适用于核退役中的实际应用。

Abstract: This paper introduces a low-cost experimental mockup to simulate the laser cutting process of containers in nuclear decommissioning. It is composed of a three-axis table supporting a cuboid container with ultraviolet-sensitive faces, a six-degree-of-freedom serial manipulator holding an ultraviolet torch that simulates the laser, and a visual system based on cameras and fiducial markers. The system employs a constrained task-space adaptive motion controller that compensates for inaccurate parameters and eliminates the need to calibrate the system. Furthermore, as the motion controller explicitly accounts for geometric constraints, the robot reactively avoids collisions with obstacles while handling the ultraviolet torch. To enhance tracking of the laser-cutting path, we control the ultraviolet beam, which requires only four degrees of freedom, instead of the full end-effector pose. Experiments show that, despite an initially uncalibrated system, the overall system is capable of tracking different trajectories with an overall mean accuracy of 3.9 (sd 2.5) mm when the end-effector pose is controlled and 2.4 (sd 1.3) mm when the ultraviolet beam is controlled.

</details>


### [112] [Towards Backdoor-Based Ownership Verification for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09005)
*Ming Sun,Rui Wang,Xingrui Yu,Lihua Jing,Hangyu Du,Zhenglin Wan,Xu Pan,Ivor Tsang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了GuardVLA，首个针对视觉-语言-动作模型（VLAs）的后门式所有权验证框架，通过在训练时将隐蔽的水印嵌入具身视觉数据中，并设计“替换-检测”机制实现模型所有权验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着VLAs模型被广泛共享和迁移，保护模型所有权对于安全部署和负责任的开源使用变得至关重要。

Method: 提出GuardVLA框架，在训练过程中通过向视觉输入注入秘密信息嵌入隐蔽且无害的后门水印；发布后采用‘替换-检测’机制，利用触发投影器和外部分类头根据预测概率激活并检测水印。

Result: 在多个数据集、模型架构和适应场景下的实验表明，GuardVLA能在不影响正常任务性能的前提下实现可靠的所有权验证，且水印在模型发布后经过微调仍可被检测。

Conclusion: GuardVLA是首个专为VLAs设计的后门水印框架，能够有效保护模型所有权，具备鲁棒性和实用性，适用于开放共享与商业化场景。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) support generalist robotic control by enabling end-to-end decision policies directly from multi-modal inputs. As trained VLAs are increasingly shared and adapted, protecting model ownership becomes essential for secure deployment and responsible open-source usage. In this paper, we present GuardVLA, the first backdoor-based ownership verification framework specifically designed for VLAs. GuardVLA embeds a stealthy and harmless backdoor watermark into the protected model during training by injecting secret messages into embodied visual data. For post-release verification, we propose a swap-and-detect mechanism, in which the trigger projector and an external classifier head are used to activate and detect the embedded backdoor based on prediction probabilities. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, model architectures, and adaptation settings demonstrate that GuardVLA enables reliable ownership verification while preserving benign task performance. Further results show that the embedded watermark remains detectable under post-release model adaptation.

</details>


### [113] [Terminal Matters: Kinodynamic Planning with a Terminal Cost and Learned Uncertainty in Belief State-Cost Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09046)
*Zhuoyun Zhong,Seyedali Golestaneh,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于动力学规划的终端成本公式，能够在优化轨迹累积成本的同时优化终端状态质量，从而提高在不确定性条件下的目标到达成功率。所提出的KiTe规划器结合学习到的动力学与不确定性模型，在多个仿真和真实任务中表现出更优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于采样的动力学规划方法通常只优化轨迹累积成本，并将目标到达视为可行性检查，而未显式优化终端状态的质量（如目标偏好或到达可靠性），这在存在不确定性的真实场景中限制了性能。

Method: 引入一种新的终端成本形式化方法，扩展至信念空间并证明最小化终端信念与目标之间的Wasserstein距离可提升到达目标区域概率的下界；提出KiTe规划器，结合学习得到的动力学和过程不确定性模型进行规划。

Result: 在Flappy Bird、Car Parking和Planar Pushing等任务上的实验表明，KiTe在不确定性条件下显著提高了目标到达成功率；真实世界平面推动实验验证了其使用学习模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过联合优化累积成本与终端状态质量，提升了动力学规划在不确定环境中的可靠性，且支持无解析不确定性模型的系统，具有良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: In many real-world robotic tasks, robots must generate dynamically feasible motions that reliably reach desired goals even under uncertainty. Yet existing sampling-based kinodynamic planners typically optimize accumulated trajectory costs and treat goal reaching as a feasibility check, rather than explicitly optimizing terminal-state quality, such as goal preference or goal-reaching reliability. In this work, we introduce a terminal-cost formulation for kinodynamic planning that allows terminal-state quality to be optimized alongside accumulated trajectory cost. We prove that AO-RRT, an asymptotically optimal kinodynamic planner, preserves its asymptotic optimality under this augmented objective. We further extend the formulation to belief space and prove that minimizing the Wasserstein distance between the terminal belief and the goal improves a lower bound on the probability of reaching the goal region. The resulting planner, KiTe, uses this terminal-cost objective to encode goal preferences and improve reliability under uncertainty. To support systems without analytical uncertainty models, we learn dynamics and process uncertainty directly from data and integrate the learned belief dynamics into planning. Experiments on Flappy Bird, Car Parking, and Planar Pushing show that KiTe consistently improves goal-reaching success under uncertainty. Real-world Planar Pushing experiments further demonstrate that KiTe can plan effectively with learned dynamics and uncertainty. Source code is available at https://github.com/elpis-lab/KiTe.

</details>


### [114] [Automated Robotic Moisture Monitoring in Agricultural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09050)
*Senthil Palanisamy,Akila I. S*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本项目提出了一种结合机器人和土壤湿度传感器的自动化农业湿度监测系统，通过Dijkstra算法规划路径并利用图像处理技术评估田地湿度。


<details>
